### [72] [A Sparse Corner-Difference MILP for Minimum Square Tiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21453)
*Zhuo Yu,Yuan Wang,Zhuo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出角差分MILP(CD-MILP)求解N×N正方形用整数边长小方块最小化铺砖数，将非零系数从Θ(N^5)降至Θ(N^3)，并加轻量角排序约束去D4对称，实验求解数优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基于放置的精确覆盖MILP含Θ(N^5)非零系数，且受正方形域二面体对称性影响，求解效率低。

Method: CD-MILP用至多四个带符号角系数表示每个所选方块；通过二维前缀重构证明约束与原单元格覆盖方程等价，覆盖相关非零数降至Θ(N^3)；并引入轻量角排序约束，在每个D4轨道中保留至少一个代表。

Result: 在9个素数尺寸实例上比较基线、CD-MILP、含角排序基线及组合，用Gurobi和COPT：组合式用Gurobi求解8个、COPT求解7个，基线仅4个和5个；平均预解矩阵非零系数约10^5，基线约10^7。

Conclusion: CD-MILP结合角排序显著减少矩阵稀疏度并提升求解能力，但并列可能残留对称性。

Abstract: We study the problem of tiling an $N\times N$ square with axis-aligned squares whose side lengths are integers strictly less than $N$, with the objective of minimizing the number of tiles. The standard placement-based exact-cover MILP contains $Θ(N^5)$ placement-to-cell nonzero coefficients and is also affected by the dihedral symmetry of the square domain. We introduce a Corner-Difference MILP (CD-MILP) that represents each selected square by at most four signed corner coefficients. Two-dimensional prefix reconstruction shows that the resulting constraints are equivalent to the original cell-cover equations, while reducing the coverage-related nonzero count to $Θ(N^3)$. We also introduce lightweight corner-ordering constraints that retain at least one representative from every $D_4$ orbit, although ties may leave residual symmetry. Computational experiments on nine prime-size instances compare the baseline, CD-MILP, the baseline with corner ordering, and their combination using Gurobi and COPT. Under the stated experimental settings, the combined formulation solves eight instances with Gurobi and seven with COPT, compared with four and five, respectively, for the baseline. Its average presolved matrixs contain approximately $10^5$ nonzero coefficients on average, compared with approximately $10^7$ for the baseline.

</details>


### [73] [Single-Loop Stochastic Projected Damped Extragradient Methods for Stochastic Nonconvex--(Strongly) Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21747)
*Huiling Zhang,Minhao Zhang,Zi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出单循环随机投影阻尼外推梯度法(SPDE)及其递归方差缩减变体(VR-SPDE)，用于随机非凸-(强)凹极小极大优化，在博弈稳定性和优化稳定性两方面给出复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机非凸-(强)凹极小极大优化中，需要同时保证博弈稳定性(GS)与优化稳定性(OS)的复杂度，且已有最优界多依赖多循环方法。作者希望以单循环结构达到甚至超越这些界。

Method: 提出SPDE（随机投影阻尼外推梯度）与VR-SPDE（递归方差缩减变体），均保持单循环结构；在无偏有界方差随机梯度预言机下分析SPDE，在额外均方Lipschitz条件下分析VR-SPDE。

Result: GS复杂度：SPDE在非凸-强凹/非凸-凹下为O(κε^{-4})和O(ε^{-5})，VR-SPDE改进为O(κ^{3/2}ε^{-3})和O(ε^{-9/2})。OS复杂度：SPDE为O(κε^{-4})和O(ε^{-6})，VR-SPDE为O(κ^{3/2}ε^{-3})和O(ε^{-6})。OS界与最优多循环方法匹配。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持单循环实现的同时，在两个问题类别和两种稳定性准则下提供了单循环随机一阶方法中已知最优的SFO复杂度保证。

Abstract: We develop single-loop stochastic projected damped extragradient methods for stochastic nonconvex--(strongly) concave minimax optimization, with complexity guarantees for both game stationarity (GS) and optimization stationarity (OS). Our approach combines a stochastic projected damped extragradient (SPDE) method with a recursive variance-reduced variant, VR-SPDE, both of which retain a single-loop structure. Under an unbiased stochastic gradient oracle with uniformly bounded variance, SPDE finds an $\varepsilon$-game-stationary point with stochastic first-order oracle (SFO) complexities of $O(κ\varepsilon^{-4})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-5})$ in the nonconvex--strongly concave and nonconvex--concave settings, respectively, where $κ=L/μ$. Under an additional mean-square Lipschitz condition on the stochastic gradients, VR-SPDE improves these GS complexities to $O(κ^{3/2}\varepsilon^{-3})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-9/2})$, respectively. For an $\varepsilon$-optimization-stationary point, SPDE achieves SFO complexities of $O(κ\varepsilon^{-4})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-6})$, while VR-SPDE achieves $O(κ^{3/2}\varepsilon^{-3})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-6})$, in the two settings, respectively. These OS guarantees match the best-known bounds achieved by multi-loop methods while preserving a single-loop implementation. To the best of our knowledge, our results provide the best-known SFO complexity guarantees among single-loop stochastic first-order methods for the respective stationarity criteria and problem classes.

</details>


### [74] [Distributed Intraday Energy Management for 16.7-Hz Railway Power Systems---Part II: Software Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21802)
*Navid Noroozi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文是Part I数学模型的Python实现（Part II），给出两层能量管理系统的可执行软件，涵盖时刻表/价格/日前/日内四层，并详述数据契约、求解与审计机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part I 只给出两层能量管理系统的数学表述，需要可执行、可审计的实现来支撑工程应用与后续性能评估（Part III）。

Method: 时刻表处理层将小时级铁路数据转为15分钟并计算牵引与再生功率；价格处理层通过缓存或ENTSO-E接口获取电价；日前规划层构建确定性小时级计划（must-run或次小时可行性证书两种模式）；日内层用风险中性场景MPC，按区域二次规划+consensus ADMM求解；S1预测层采用一周季节朴素点预测加因果残差路径重采样。

Result: 实现规范了日前/日内数据契约、绝对UTC耦合、局部QP组装、热启动、残差检验、求解器状态解释、物理验证的恢复路径、状态与KPI更新及铁路自有发电/抽水增量；并通过版本、依赖锁、冻结计划清单、确定性夹具、运行日志与哈希清单保证可执行可审计。

Conclusion: 本文聚焦软件行为与数值契约，验证其可实现性与可审计性；对比性能与压力测试结论留待Part III。

Abstract: This paper presents the executable Python implementation~\cite{noroozi2026bahnstromems} of the two-layer energy-management system developed mathematically in Part~I~\cite{norooziP1I}. If needed, the software first converts hourly railway operation data into a 15-minute timetable and calculates the amount of gross motoring and available regenerative powers in a 15-minute resolution using the timetable handling layer. Given the energy prices are available either cached or by sending request to ENTSO-E Transparency Platform through the price handling layer, the software constructs a deterministic hourly day-ahead plan with either in the so-called must-run converter commitment mode or in a sub-hourly feasibility certificate mode, where the latter demands more computational complexity. Given the inputs from the timetable, day-ahead planning layers and initial measurements of the intraday-resolutions network's physical quantities, the intraday handling layer executes a risk-neutral intraday scenario model predictive controller by area-wise quadratic programming and consensus ADMM. The retained S1 forecast layer combines a one-week seasonal-naive point forecast with causal residual-path resampling, while the current measured stage is identical in all scenarios. We specify the day-ahead and intraday data contracts, absolute-UTC coupling, local-QP assembly, warm starts, residual tests, solver-status interpretation, physically validated recovery paths, state and key-performance-index updates, and the railway-owned generation and pumping increment. The Python package version, dependency lock, frozen-plan manifests, deterministic fixtures, run logs, and hash manifests make the software configuration executable and auditable. The paper describes software behavior and numerical contracts; comparative performance and stress-test claims are reserved for Part~III~\cite{norooziP1III}.

</details>


### [75] [Distributed Intraday Energy Management for 16.7-Hz Railway Power Systems---Part I: Theoretical Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21808)
*Navid Noroozi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文是16.7 Hz单相铁路供电系统日内能量管理分布式场景规划器系列三篇论文的第一篇，建立了双层EMS的数学基础，将日内能量规划建模为风险中性凸随机优化问题，并通过分布式场景MPC递归求解，同时提出从时刻表估计微观牵引/再生功率剖面的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 16.7 Hz单相铁路电网能量管理需要日内规划能力，但真实网络的详细列车运动与微观牵引/再生功率数据因隐私和安全原因通常不可公开，给精确仿真与数值验证带来困难。

Method: 构建由多个控制区互联组成的16.7 Hz单相铁路电网日内时间尺度数学模型；将日内能量规划表述为风险中性凸随机优化问题（稀疏凸二次规划），采用分布式场景MPC递归求解，并推导各区子问题、协调更新、收敛条件及闭环KPI累积；另提出将因果时刻表与列车运动信息转换为15分钟牵引/再生功率剖面且严格保持各小时能量目标的估计方法。

Result: 日内规划被证明为稀疏凸二次规划，可通过分布式场景MPC求解；实现了开源Python包bahnstrom_ems v0.9.1；提出了保持小时能量目标的微观功率剖面估计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为16.7 Hz铁路供电系统的分布式日内能量管理奠定了数学基础，兼顾数据隐私限制，后续论文将基于此实现并测试开源工具。

Abstract: In a series of three papers, we propose a distributed intraday scenario planner for energy management in 16.7-Hz single-phase railway power systems, which is realized and tested as open-source Python package \texttt{bahnstrom\_ems v0.9.1}~\cite{noroozi2026bahnstromems}. The first part of our work develops the mathematical foundation of a two-layer energy management system for a 16.7-Hz single-phase railway power network, where the network is an interconnection of several control areas. Given a day-ahead plan, last measurement of stored energy in wayside batteries, and forecasts of the tractive, available regenerative and renewable energy, we provide a mathematical modeling of the 16.7-Hz single-phase railway power network in intraday time-scale. Then the intraday energy planning is formulated as a risk-neutral convex stochastic optimization problem. The overall intraday planning is shown to be a sparse convex quadratic program. Hence, this problem is solved recursively by adopting a distributed scenario model predictive control (MPC) setting. The corresponding area problems, coordination updates, convergence conditions, and closed-loop key-performance-index accumulation are derived. In practice, detailed train-motion and microscopic motoring and regenerative-power profiles are usually needed for accurate simulations and numerical validation purposes. Due to data privacy and security concerns, detailed train-motion and microscopic motoring and regenerative-power profiles in a real power network may not be publicly available. Therefore, we also propose a mathematically-solid approach to estimate such microscopic information by converting causally timetable and train-motion information into quarter-hour motoring and regenerative-power profiles while exactly preserving each hourly energy target.

</details>


### [76] [A Tight Surrogate-Based Adaptive Step Size for the Multiobjective Conditional Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21831)
*Wang Chen,Yong Zhao,Liping Tang,Xinmin Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多目标条件梯度（MCondG）方法中自适应步长过于保守的问题，提出利用各目标梯度Lipschitz常数的更紧二次代理函数，并在有限候选集上评估，得到改进的自适应步长策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCondG原自适应步长通过最小化一维凸二次代理函数获得，但该代理函数由所有目标函数梯度的最大Lipschitz常数主导，较为保守，导致步长过小、收敛变慢。

Method: 提出利用各目标函数各自的梯度Lipschitz常数的更紧二次代理函数，并基于该代理函数在有限候选集上评估，设计新的自适应步长策略。

Result: 证明了新步长保持MCondG的下降性质，并相比原自适应步长策略具有更优的最坏情况复杂度界；在基准和真实问题上的数值实验表明该方法在计算效率和解质量上更有效。

Conclusion: 所提步长策略能缓解保守性、加速收敛，并保持理论保证，在实践中表现更优。

Abstract: The multiobjective conditional gradient (MCondG) method is an effective descent-type algorithm for solving constrained multiobjective optimization problems. In this algorithm, the adaptive step size is derived by minimizing a one-dimensional convex quadratic surrogate function, which guarantees a sufficient decrease in all objective functions at each iteration. However, we observe that this surrogate function is relatively conservative, producing overly small step sizes and consequently slowing the convergence of the algorithm. This conservativeness stems from the fact that the surrogate function is dominated by the largest Lipschitz constant among the gradients of all objective functions. Motivated by this observation, we develop a tighter quadratic surrogate function that exploits the individual gradient Lipschitz constants. Based on this surrogate, we propose a new adaptive step size strategy obtained by evaluating this surrogate over a finite candidate set. We prove that the proposed step size preserves the descent property of the MCondG method, and has an improved worst-case complexity bound compared with the original adaptive step size strategy. Numerical experiments on benchmark and real-world problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed strategy in terms of computational efficiency and solution quality.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Shadow Weights: Quantization-Aware Training as Quantized-Endpoint Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21834)
*Sheng-An Xu,Hanyang Li,Jianhao Ma,Ying Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将QAT重新解释为有限网格上的端点动力学，提出基于残差相位的理想化模型与"穿越律"，并据此设计直接作用于量化码的QAR框架，在无需保存全精度影子权重的情况下实现与QAT相当或更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对QAT成功的解释多从影子权重视角出发（平坦盆地、量化振荡鲁棒性、影子损失与量化误差的平衡），但这些视角无法直接解释一个关键经验现象：部署端点损失 f(Q(x)) 会改善，而影子损失 f(x) 并不改善甚至显著上升。因此需要一种新的解释框架。

Method: 受预训练权重缩放后近似正态性的启发，提出针对残差相位的理想化模型——将每个影子权重在其量化单元内的位置表示为单元宽度的比例，由此导出"穿越律"，判定影子更新后哪些坐标会跨越量化边界。基于该模型及QAT中的信号不平衡现象，进一步提出QAR框架，直接操作量化码而非维护影子权重。

Result: QAR在理论上可为一类幂放大函数给出可行梯度界（受限于不可避免的有限网格下界），且内存高效，无需保留全精度影子权重副本。大语言模型后训练实验证据与端点视角一致，QAR在更小内存开销下可媲美甚至优于QAT。

Conclusion: 将QAT视为有限网格端点动力学能更直接地解释其部署端点的改善现象，并催生出理论有据、内存高效的新框架QAR，为量化训练提供了新的视角与方法。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) updates a full-precision shadow weight $\mathbf{x}$ but deploys the quantized endpoint $Q(\mathbf{x})$. Existing explanations for QAT largely view its success through the lens of shadow weights: QAT can move $\mathbf{x}$ toward flatter basins, gain robustness from quantization-induced oscillations, or balance the shadow loss $f(\mathbf{x})$ against the quantization error $\|\mathbf{x}-Q(\mathbf{x})\|_2$. These perspectives do not directly explain the empirical observation that the deployed endpoint loss $f(Q(\mathbf{x}))$ improves while the shadow loss $f(\mathbf{x})$ does not, and can even increase substantially. In this paper, we offer a different explanation by treating QAT as finite-grid endpoint dynamics. Motivated by the approximate normality of rescaled pretrained weights, we propose an idealized model for the residual phase, which records where each shadow weight sits inside its quantization cell as a fraction of the cell width. This model leads to a crossing law that determines which coordinates cross quantization boundaries after a shadow update. Inspired by the idealized model and signal-imbalance phenomenon in QAT, we further propose QAR (Quantization with Amplified Routing), an algorithmic framework that directly operates on the quantization code. In contrast to QAT, QAR is both theoretically grounded and memory-efficient: it admits feasible-gradient bounds for a family of power amplifiers up to unavoidable finite-grid floors without retaining a full-precision shadow weight copy. Experiments on post-training of large language models provide evidence consistent with the endpoint view and show that QAR can be comparable to or better than QAT with smaller memory cost.

</details>


### [78] [Application of Optimal Inexact Second-Order Acceleration to Distributed Stochastic Optimization under Statistical Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21878)
*Yury A. Sokolov,Maxim K. Mashtaler,Alexander V. Gasnikov,Martin Takáč,Dmitry I. Kamzolov,Artem D. Agafonov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究分布式随机凸优化，提出一种分布式重启加速不精确牛顿外推梯度方法，仅通信梯度、不通信Hessian，达到统计精度 N^{-1/2} 仅需 Õ(max{N^{1/7}, m^{1/4}}) 通信轮数，改进此前 N^{1/6} 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定 N 个样本分布于 m 个worker的分布式随机凸优化中，通信轮数是主要瓶颈。此前加速三次牛顿方法需 Õ(N^{1/6}) 轮，样本量依赖偏高，且Hessian通信代价大。作者希望利用样本平均近似下局部Hessian统计相似的性质，在不通信Hessian的前提下降低通信复杂度。

Method: 将样本平均近似问题化为正则化有限和问题，利用局部Hessian的统计相似性，把服务器端局部目标的Hessian当作全局目标的不精确Hessian，worker只需通信梯度。采用(Chen et al., 2026)的最优加速不精确牛顿外推梯度法，并针对强凸经验问题提出其分布式重启变体。每次迭代使用两轮梯度聚合。

Result: 达到统计精度 N^{-1/2} 的通信轮数为 Õ(max{N^{1/7}, m^{1/4}})。当 m=N^{4/7} 时仅需 Õ(N^{1/7}) 轮，将总样本量依赖从先前方法的 Õ(N^{1/6}) 改进到 Õ(N^{1/7})，且无需Hessian通信。

Conclusion: 该分布式重启加速不精确牛顿外推梯度方法在仅通信梯度的条件下显著降低了通信轮数对样本量的依赖，是分布式随机凸优化中通信效率的重要改进。

Abstract: We consider distributed stochastic convex optimization with a fixed budget of $N$ independent samples split among $m$ workers. Sample average approximation reduces the problem to a regularized finite-sum problem whose local Hessians are statistically similar. This allows the Hessian of the local objective at the server to be used as an inexact Hessian of the global objective, while the workers communicate only gradients. We apply the optimal accelerated inexact Newton extragradient method of (Chen et al., 2026) and propose its distributed restarted variant for the strongly convex empirical problem. The method reaches the statistical accuracy of order $N^{-1/2}$ in $\widetilde O\left(\max\{N^{1/7},m^{1/4}\}\right)$ communication rounds. Hence, with $m=N^{4/7}$ workers, it requires $\widetilde O\left(N^{1/7}\right)$ rounds, improving the dependence on the total sample size from $\widetilde O\left(N^{1/6}\right)$ for the previous accelerated cubic Newton construction of (Agafonov et al., 2021). Each iteration uses two gradient aggregation rounds and does not require Hessian communication.

</details>


### [79] [Near-Optimal Acceleration for Smooth $\ell_p$ / $\ell_q$ Nondual Convex First-Order Oracle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21880)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Brian Bullins,Cristóbal Guzmán,Mathieu Molina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\ell_q$ 球上具有 $(L,\kappa-1)$-Hölder 连续梯度的凸优化问题，通过结合嵌套凸集追踪的 selector 移动界与 Hölder 下降，给出多项式时间一阶方法，在高维 $T\le d$ 情形下达到 $\widetilde O(LR^\kappa/T^{\kappa(1+1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+)-1})$ 的误差率，解决了 COLT 2015 公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Guz15 在 COLT 2015 提出的公开问题：在 $\ell_q$ 球约束下、梯度仅为 Hölder 连续（而非 Lipschitz）时，如何获得（近）最优的一阶方法收敛率。Lipschitz 凸优化已有方法被归约到 selector 移动界，但需要将其推广到 Hölder 连续梯度情形。

Method: 利用 (MG26) 提供的 selectors，对高维嵌套凸集追踪问题在任意 $p<q$ 下给出移动界，并将 Lipschitz 凸优化归约为 selector 移动界。作者将该移动界与 Hölder 下降耦合，构造多项式时间一阶方法，分析 $T$ 次一阶 oracle 查询后的可行输出误差。

Result: 在高维 regime $T\le d$ 且 $p<\min\{q,2\}$ 下，$T$ 次查询后误差为 $\widetilde O_{\kappa,p,q}(LR^\kappa/T^{\kappa(1+1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+)-1})$，达到 Guz15 公开问题的最优率（忽略对数因子）。当 $(p,q)=(1,2)$ 时，率为 $\widetilde O(LR^\kappa/T^{2\kappa-1})$；光滑情形 $\kappa=2$ 时为 $\widetilde O(LR^2/T^3)$ 的三次衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作给出统一框架，将凸嵌套集追踪的 selector 移动界与 Hölder 下降结合，解决了 Hölder 连续梯度凸优化的 COLT 2015 公开问题，并在 $\ell_2$ 情形获得含三次衰减的收敛率。

Abstract: We study the optimization of convex objectives with $(L,κ-1)$-Hölder-continuous gradients in $\ell_q$ over $R B_p^d$, $1<κ\le 2$. (MG26) provides selectors with a movement bound for the problem of chasing high-dimensional convex nested sets for every $p<q$ and generally reduces Lipschitz convex optimization to bounds on the movement of selectors. We couple that movement with Hölder descent yielding a polynomial-runtime first-order method whose feasible output, in the high-dimensional regime $T\le d$ and for $p<\min\{q,2\}$, has error $$
  \widetilde O_{κ,p,q}\!\left(
  \frac{LR^κ}{T^{κ(1+1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+)-1}}
  \right), $$ after $T$ queries to a first-order oracle, solving the COLT 2015 open problem of (Guz15), up to logarithmic factors. At $(p,q)=(1,2)$, the rate is $\widetilde{O}(LR^κ/T^{2κ-1})$, including $\widetilde{O}(LR^2/T^{3})$ cubic decay in the smooth case.

</details>


### [80] [An $Ω(κ_y^8ε^{-6})$ Lower Bound for Stochastic NC-SC Bilevel Optimization with First-order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21905)
*Zhihao Gu,Qilong Wu,Junchi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸上层、强凸下层的平滑双层优化问题的oracle复杂度下界，证明随机一阶算法至少需要 Ω(Δκ_y²ε⁻²max{1,σ²κ_y⁶ε⁻⁴}) 次查询，确立了已有方法 ε⁻⁶ 依赖的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化中已有随机一阶方法可达到 ε⁻⁶ 的复杂度，但其是否最优缺乏理论证明；本文旨在给出匹配的oracle复杂度下界以确认最优性。

Method: 针对上层非凸、下层强凸且满足平滑性的双层问题，考虑返回无偏随机梯度、方差为 σ² 的随机一阶oracle，构造困难实例并运用信息论/对抗性论证证明任何自适应随机算法的查询下界。

Result: 证明任何自适应随机一阶算法找到 ε-稳定点至少需 Ω(Δκ_y²ε⁻²max{1,σ²κ_y⁶ε⁻⁴}) 次查询；在噪声主导区域下界为 Ω(Δσ²κ_y⁸ε⁻⁶)，与最好已知方法的 ε⁻⁶ 依赖吻合。

Conclusion: 该下界确立了最优随机一阶方法 ε⁻⁶ 复杂度的最优性，为双层优化的算法复杂度提供了紧致刻画。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of finding $ε$-stationary points of smooth bilevel optimization problems with a nonconvex upper-level objective and a strongly convex lower-level problem. We consider a stochastic first-order oracle that returns unbiased stochastic gradients of both the upper- and lower-level objectives, with variance bounded by $σ^2$. We prove that, for any initial optimality gap $Δ>0$ and all sufficiently small $ε>0$, every adaptive randomized first-order algorithm requires $Ω\!\left(Δκ_y^2 ε^{-2}\max\{1,σ^2κ_y^6ε^{-4}\}\right)$ oracle queries to find an $ε$-stationary point of its hyper-objective function, where $κ_y$ denotes the condition number of the lower-level problem. In particular, in the noise-dominated regime, the lower bound is $Ω\!\left(Δσ^2κ_y^8ε^{-6}\right)$. This establishes the optimality of the $ε^{-6}$ dependence achieved by the best-known first-order stochastic methods.

</details>

### [81] [Your Programming Students' Cognition with ChatGPT: Higher Performance, Lower Retention, and Reduced Ownership](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21194)
*Christian Bergh,Benjamin Tag,Alexandra Vassar,Jake Renzella*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 有 ChatGPT 辅助的学生编程成绩更高，但即时与 48 小时后的回忆成绩更低，且对提交代码的归属感较弱，揭示"辅助表现"与"知识留存"之间的落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式 AI 能提升编程任务表现，但任务顺利完成未必代表学生真正掌握或记住知识，需要实证检验 AI 辅助对学习留存、认知负荷与代码归属感的影响。

Method: 59 名本科计算机专业学生（55 人纳入分析）参与受控组间实验，完成三个 C 语言入门任务，分为可使用 ChatGPT-4.5 组与仅用传统网页检索组；测量任务成绩、自评心理努力与难度、瞳孔反应、心率变异性与归属感，并在任务后即时及 48 小时后进行线索回忆测试。

Result: ChatGPT 组编程得分更高（89% vs. 69%），但即时回忆（41% vs. 53%）和 48 小时后回忆（39% vs. 52%）均更低；两组 48 小时内回忆流失量无显著差异；ChatGPT 组自评心理努力增长更少（Holm 校正 p=.047），对提交代码的自我归属比例为 45% vs. 81%。生理指标的验证性检验未发现显著差异，但数据缺失严重，结果解释受限。

Conclusion: AI 辅助表现与后续回忆及归属感之间存在明显落差，提示评估方式应要求学生解释、检索并真正参与所提交的工作，AI 学习工具设计也应促进主动学习而非仅提升任务完成度。

Abstract: Generative AI can improve students' programming performance, but successful task completion may not reflect what they retain. We examined performance, retention, cognitive load, and ownership in a controlled between-subjects experiment with 59 undergraduate computer science students, 55 were retained for analysis. Participants completed three introductory C programming tasks with access to ChatGPT-4.5 or conventional web search without generative AI. We measured task performance, self-reported mental effort and difficulty, pupillary responses, heart rate variability, and ownership, and assessed cued recall immediately and 48 hours later. ChatGPT-assisted students achieved higher coding scores (89% vs. 69%) but lower recall scores immediately (41% vs. 53%) and after 48 hours (39% vs. 52%). There was no significant difference in the loss of recall information over 48 hours between the groups. Self-reported mental effort increased less across tasks in the ChatGPT condition (Holm-adjusted p = .047), and students attributed less of the submitted code to themselves (45% vs. 81%). Confirmatory physiological tests did not detect significant differences in trajectories between conditions; substantial data loss limits their interpretation. These findings reveal a gap between assisted task performance and subsequent recall and sense of ownership in this setting. They motivate the need for assessment practices and AI learning tools that require students to explain, retrieve, and contribute to the work they submit as active participants in their education.

</details>


### [82] [Open Platform Field Experiments: Expanding the Design Space of Experimental Research on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21608)
*Jordi Guillem Condom-Tibau,Giovanni Puccetti,Clara Bacciu,Matteo Abrate,Stefano Cresci*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"开放平台田野实验"(OPFEs)这一新方法论范式，使独立研究者能在开放社交媒体（如Bluesky/AT协议）上直接干预客户端、推荐系统与审核服务等功能组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体因果证据需求增长，但独立研究者难以直接在平台上做实验；现有方法（受控调查、模拟、客户端覆盖、平台合作）各有取舍，亟需新的实验途径。

Method: 提出社交媒体实验的设计空间，梳理多种实验原型并进行比较分析；刻画支撑OPFEs的架构与治理要素，映射到Bluesky和AT协议，并展示完整OPFE设计的端到端生命周期。

Result: 比较分析显示OPFEs占据此前未被探索的设计空间区域；架构与治理要素分析表明开放平台可支撑独立研究者实施OPFE。

Conclusion: OPFEs确立了独立、透明、生态有效的开放社交媒体实验方法论范式。

Abstract: Despite a growing demand for causal evidence about social media, independent researchers remain severely constrained in their ability to conduct experiments directly on online platforms. To cope, multiple methodological workarounds have emerged - from controlled surveys and simulations to client-side overlays and platform partnerships - each requiring distinct trade-offs between desirable experimental properties. The recent emergence of open social media platforms offers a qualitatively different methodological opportunity. Here we propose a design space of social media experimentation and discuss Open Platform Field Experiments (OPFEs). OPFEs represent a distinct class of experimental approaches that enable independent researchers to directly intervene on functional platform components - such as clients, recommendation systems, and moderation services - within live social media environments. Through a comparative analysis of experimental archetypes, we show that OPFEs occupy a previously unexplored region of the design space. We then bridge theory and practice by characterizing the architectural and governance elements that enable OPFEs, mapping them onto Bluesky and the AT Protocol, and illustrating the end-to-end lifecycle of a complete OPFE design. Overall, this work establishes OPFEs as a practical methodological paradigm for independent, transparent, and ecologically grounded experimentation on open social media.

</details>


### [83] [Artificial Intelligence as an Economic, Environmental, Geopolitical, and Social Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21632)
*Marcin Marciniak*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文主张将AI视为一种重塑资本、资源与权力分配的政治经济力量，而非单纯技术突破，其社会后果取决于制度与政策选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论多把AI当作技术突破，忽视了它对资本、能源、自然资源、劳动与政治权力的再分配效应，因此需要一种政治经济学视角来审视AI。

Method: 概念性与框架性分析：沿AI价值链考察投资集中度、算力基础设施、半导体与云服务等地理集中的瓶颈，以及由此产生的地缘经济结构性权力；同时分析AI的环境双重效应与自动化对就业的影响。

Result: AI投资日益集中于少数企业与国家；数据中心扩张带来电力与水资源压力及地方分配冲突；对先进半导体、设备、云服务、能源与专业知识等瓶颈的控制可转化为不依赖军事优势的结构性权力；AI环境效应兼具消耗性与增效性；自动化在部分岗位减少就业、在另一些岗位增强劳动并创造新任务。

Conclusion: AI转型的社会结果不取决于技术本身，而取决于竞争政策、基础设施规划、环境监管、社会保护、集体谈判与生产率收益分配等制度安排。

Abstract: The rapid development of artificial intelligence is often discussed primarily as a technological breakthrough. Such an approach is insufficient because AI is also transforming the allocation of capital, energy, natural resources, labour, and political power. Investment in generative AI and computing infrastructure is increasing rapidly and is increasingly concentrated among a small number of corporations and countries. At the same time, the expansion of data centres creates new electricity and water demands, potentially producing local infrastructure bottlenecks and distributive conflicts. The AI value chain is dependent on geographically concentrated supplies of advanced semiconductors, manufacturing equipment, cloud services, energy, and specialised knowledge. Control over these bottlenecks may give states and corporations structural power that does not derive directly from military superiority. The environmental effects of AI are similarly ambivalent: AI systems consume energy, water, materials, and computing hardware, but may also improve energy efficiency, climate modelling, renewable-energy integration, and environmental monitoring. Finally, AI-enabled automation, including intelligent robotics, may reduce employment in some occupations while augmenting labour and creating new tasks in others. The social outcome of this transformation will therefore depend not only on the technology itself but also on competition policy, infrastructure planning, environmental regulation, social protection, collective bargaining, and the distribution of productivity gains.

</details>


### [84] [When AI Enters the Workplace, Who Faces Greater Risks? A Gendered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21756)
*Miriam Fernandez,Ángel Pavón Pérez,Damiano Giallongo,Davide Ghia,Maryam Yaqub,Daniele Quercia,Tania Cerquitelli*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究AI创新暴露在性别主导职业中的分布差异，发现女性主导职业的LLM暴露更高且各技能/工资层级分布均匀，低技能低工资女性职业面临更大自动化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性别不平等是劳动力市场的结构性特征。随着AI改变组织实践，人们担忧任务自动化、技能提升机会不均等和技术变革回报差异可能无意中放大现有性别差距，因此需要考察AI暴露如何在男性与女性主导职业间分布。

Method: 使用一个将职业特征与AI暴露度量相链接的新数据集，分析LLMs及更广泛AI技术如何分布于劳动力市场，重点比较男性主导与女性主导职业在技能和工资分布上的AI暴露差异。

Result: 男性主导职业的AI暴露集中在高技能高工资职业；女性主导职业的暴露在高技能高工资与低技能低工资职业间相对均匀。LLM相关暴露在女性主导职业中更高，而更广泛的AI创新暴露更集中于男性主导职业。

Conclusion: 结合现有文献三角验证表明，女性尤其是处于最脆弱位置（低技能低工资女性主导职业）者，可能面临更高的任务自动化、工作重组、工资下降和职业晋升受限等AI相关风险。

Abstract: Gender inequality remains a persistent structural feature of the labour market, shaping women's lifetime earnings and economic security. As artificial intelligence (AI) transforms organisational practices, there is growing concern that existing disparities may be unintentionally amplified through task automation, unequal access to upskilling opportunities, and differential returns obtained from technological change. In this paper, we examine how exposure to AI-driven innovation varies across male- and female-dominated occupations, with particular attention to differences across the skill and wage distribution. Using a novel dataset that links occupational characteristics to measures of AI exposure, we analyse how recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and broader AI technologies are distributed across the labour market. Our findings show that, while AI exposure is generally concentrated in higher-skilled and higher-paid occupations for male-dominated occupations, female-dominated occupations display relatively uniform levels of exposure across both high-skilled, high-paid, and low-skilled, low-paid occupations. Moreover, we find that LLM-related exposure is higher in female-dominated occupations, while exposure to broader AI innovation remains more concentrated in male-dominated occupations. A triangulation of these results with existing literature suggests that women, particularly those in the most vulnerable positions (lower-skilled and lower-paid female-dominated occupations), may face greater exposure to forms of AI associated with task automation, job restructuring, reduction of wages and limited career progression.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond the Desert Label: A Pathway Diagnostic for User-Centered Smart Mobility Service Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21956)
*Oluwasegun Adegoke,Sevgi Erdogan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于路径、可复现、数据驱动的诊断方法，用于区分"相对公交错配"与"最低服务缺失"两种公交荒漠问题，并识别导致分类的具体服务属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能城市出行平台常用空间筛查工具识别公交服务无法满足依赖型用户的社区，但单一的"公交荒漠"标签会掩盖两类性质不同的用户问题：服务与集中需求之间的局部错配，以及可用服务的基本缺失。二者需要不同的以用户为中心的应对策略。

Method: 构建结合开放数据（GTFS、ACS、LEHD、Census、OpenStreetMap）的工作流，使用Local Moran's I检测空间连贯的服务错配，并应用以弱势用户为中心的、体现公平的服务缺失检验，报告驱动分类的具体服务属性（发车频率、服务时段、周末服务、步行可达性、目的地可达性）。

Result: 在巴尔的摩、费城、纳什维尔和达拉斯应用中显示：传统公交城市以局部错配为主，而汽车导向型城市表现为更广泛的最低服务缺失，且各案例拥有不同的服务赤字特征。

Conclusion: 通过明确标示欠服务标签背后的机制，该工具支持在不同公交基线的城市中开展更具包容性、以用户为中心的智能出行规划。

Abstract: Smart-city mobility platforms increasingly rely on spatial screening tools to identify neighborhoods where public transit fails dependent users, but a single transit desert label can mask very different user problems: localized mismatch between service and concentrated need, or basic absence of usable service. These call for different user-centered responses. This paper introduces a pathway-based, reproducible, data-driven diagnostic that distinguishes relative transit mismatch from minimum-service failure and reports the specific service attributes (frequency, span, weekend service, walking access, and destination accessibility) driving each classification. The workflow combines open data (GTFS, ACS, LEHD, Census, and OpenStreetMap), detects spatially coherent mismatch using Local Moran's I, and applies an equity-informed service-failure test that centers vulnerable users. Applied to Baltimore, Philadelphia, Nashville, and Dallas, the diagnostic shows that legacy-transit cities are dominated by localized mismatch, while auto-oriented cities show broader minimum-service failure, with distinct service-deficit profiles in each case. By making the mechanism behind an under-service label explicit, the tool supports more inclusive, user-centered smart-mobility planning across cities with different transit baselines.

</details>

### [86] [Efficient simulation schemes for pricing options under the Ornstein--Uhlenbeck driven stochastic volatility model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21291)
*Congxin He,Yue Kuen Kwok*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于算子分裂的蒙特卡洛模拟方法，用于Ornstein-Uhlenbeck随机波动率模型下的期权定价，避免条件积分方差抽样和特征函数逆变换两大数值难点，并在精度-速度权衡上优于三种前沿方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OU驱动的随机波动率模型下传统模拟方法面临两大数值挑战：条件积分方差的抽样，以及特征函数的路径逆积分变换，这些难点限制了模拟效率与精度。

Method: 通过巧妙的算子分裂技术将控制随机微分方程分解，使每个子步骤都有解析解，模拟仅需生成少量正态随机变量，从而绕开上述两大数值难点；并结合鞅保持控制变量和条件方差缩减技术。

Result: 与三种前沿方案（Zeng等2023的Hilbert插值、Choi 2025的Karhunen-Loève展开、Brignone和Sgarra 2026基于逆高斯矩匹配）相比，本方案在精度-速度权衡上表现良好，尤其适合具有大量监控时点的路径依赖期权定价。

Conclusion: 算子分裂方案为OU随机波动率模型的期权定价提供了一种高效且精确的蒙特卡洛模拟途径，结合控制变量与方差缩减后可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: We develop an efficient Monte Carlo simulation scheme for pricing options under the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck driven stochastic volatility model via the operator splitting approach. With an ingenious splitting of the governing stochastic differential equations, our operator splitting scheme admits analytic solutions in all sub-steps, so its implementation is simplified to require simulation of a few normal variates. This resolves the two typical numerical challenges in other simulation schemes, namely, sampling of conditional integrated variance and pathwise inverse integral transform of characteristic functions. There are three pioneering simulation schemes that attempt to overcome the above two numerical challenges. These include the Hilbert interpolation scheme of Zeng et al. (2023), Karhunen-Lo`eve expansion scheme of Choi (2025) and moment matching scheme based on the Inverse Gaussian distribution of Brignone and Sgarra (2026). We performed numerical tests to compare accuracy-speed performance of pricing options using our operator splitting scheme with these three pioneering schemes. We found that our scheme competes favorably well in terms of accuracy-speed tradeoff among all these schemes, in particular for pricing path dependent options with a large number of monitoring instants. The performance of our scheme can be well enhanced by martingale-preserving control variates and variance reduction via conditioning.

</details>


### [87] [Simulation of stochastic volatility models via operator splitting schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21301)
*Lilian Hu,Congxin He,Yue Kuen Kwok,Gongqiu Zhang*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Strang算子分裂的通用模拟框架，用于随机波动率模型的期权定价，避免条件积分方差评估，精度与效率优于现有精确模拟和Euler格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Euler离散化在随机波动率模型下存在高偏差和不可靠性；精确（无偏）模拟需数值评估条件于终端方差的条件积分方差，通常依赖繁琐的Fourier反演或矩匹配近似，技术难度大。

Method: 利用Strang算子分裂近似构建高效可靠的模拟方案，完全规避条件积分方差（和/或波动率）的评估需求，并给出二阶收敛性证明。

Result: 方案在精度、效率、可靠性和易实现性上优于多数现有精确模拟方案及有偏Euler方案；在Heston类、lifted Heston、Hull-White及Barndorff-Nielsen和Shephard模型上进行了大量数值测试验证通用性。

Conclusion: 算子分裂方法为随机波动率模型期权定价提供了一种通用、高效且可靠的模拟框架，并具二阶收敛性保证。

Abstract: The standard Euler discretization schemes for numerical option pricing under stochastic volatility models are known to exhibit high biases and potential unreliability. The alternative use of the exact (unbiased) simulation approach invariably involves numerical evaluation of integrated variance (and / or volatility) conditional on terminal variance (volatility) value. To resolve the technical challenge, most simulation schemes either employ the tedious Fourier inversion of conditional characteristic function or numerical approximation by moment matched distribution. We propose a general framework of constructing efficient and reliable simulation schemes for stochastic volatility models via the Strang operator splitting approximation. The simulation procedure completely circumvents the necessity of evaluation of conditional integrated variance (and / or) volatility. Our simulation schemes compete favorably well with most existing exact simulation schemes and the biased Euler schemes in terms of accuracy, efficiency, reliability and ease of implementation. Extensive numerical tests were conducted to illustrate the versatility and success of our operator splitting approach for most common stochastic volatility models, such as the Heston-type models, lifted Heston model, Hull-White model, and Barndorff-Nielsen and Shephard model. We also establish the proof of second-order convergence of the operator splitting schemes.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.PR'></div>

### [88] [Design and pricing of a transparent parametric-modeled loss CAT bond: application to German windstorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22052)
*John Ery,Erwan Koch*

Main category: q-fin.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型巨灾债券，采用从成本随机场导出的透明触发机制，在道德风险与基差风险之间灵活权衡，并提供定价方法，以德国历史风暴事件为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨灾债券可弥补再保险容量不足，但存在道德风险与基差风险之间的权衡，且资产类别透明度不足成为市场参与障碍。气候变化下灾害频发加剧了该需求。

Method: 构建成本随机场，分离物理灾害、脆弱性函数与暴露度；触发机制可在参数化与模型化损失之间灵活取值；对德国历史风暴，用风速数据拟合标准分辨率的最大稳定随机场，并利用行业损失与暴露数据校准脆弱性；同时给出定价方法并度量基差风险、进行完整模型评估。

Result: 基于德国历史风暴事件的案例研究表明，该触发机制能在保证透明度的同时实现参数化与模型化损失之间的灵活形式，并通过校准与模型评估验证其可行性与基差风险表现。

Conclusion: 该新型巨灾债券对保险公司、公共机构及欲进入巨灾债券市场的参与者具有价值，提供了一种透明、易评估且可定价的触发方案。

Abstract: Catastrophe (cat) bonds overcome some lack of reinsurance by sourcing capacity from the wider capital markets. We present a new type of cat bond addressing the known trade-off between moral hazard and basis risk. As our main contributions we propose a trigger mechanism which is entirely transparent and simpler to evaluate compared to indemnity modeling techniques, as well as a methodology to price this cat bond. This is relevant for insurers and public authorities in a world where natural disasters are occurring with increasing frequency and severity due to climate change, but also for players willing to enter the cat bond market for whom the lack of transparency of this asset class has been a significant obstacle. Our trigger is derived from a cost random field which separates the physical hazard, a vulnerability function and the exposure. This allows the trigger to take a flexible form between parametric and modeled loss, in case exposure is taken into account. We present a case study based on historical windstorm events impacting Germany. Using wind speed data from historical storms, we fit a max-stable random field on a resolution which is standard in the reinsurance industry. The availability of industry loss and exposure data allows us to calibrate the vulnerability component to historical observations. Besides measuring the basis risk associated with our trigger, we perform a full model assessment and discuss numerical results.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Poisson Regression under Multivariate Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21056)
*Kirill O. Morozov*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出首个支持任意数量选择方程的多元样本选择Poisson回归模型，推导多元选择修正项，证明参数识别，并提出NLS与PPML两步估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Poisson样本选择模型通常仅支持单一选择方程，无法处理多个潜在相关的选择条件同时决定结果是否被观测的情形。

Method: 在结果误差与选择误差联合正态假设下推导观测结果的條件均值与多元选择修正项；证明参数识别，并表明在一定支撑与秩条件下无需排他性约束即可识别结果参数；提出基于非线性最小二乘(NLS)和Poisson伪极大似然(PPML)的两步估计，首次将PPML应用于含样本选择的Poisson模型，并给出稳健两步三明治协方差矩阵；用因子矩恢复潜在结果误差方差及结果与选择误差的相关性。

Result: 证明两种估计量的一致性；Monte Carlo模拟显示忽略样本选择在误差相关时导致持续偏差，PPML大幅降低偏差，在中强选择相依下比NLS更稳定。

Conclusion: 该模型与PPML估计方法能有效处理多元样本选择问题，减少偏差且更稳健，具有理论与实证价值。

Abstract: This paper develops a Poisson regression model with multivariate sample selection, in which the outcome is observed only when several potentially correlated selection conditions are satisfied. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first Poisson sample selection model that allows for an arbitrary number of selection equations. We derive the conditional mean of the observed outcome under joint normality of the outcome and selection errors and obtain a multivariate selection-correction term.
  We prove identification of the model parameters and show that, under suitable support and rank conditions, the outcome parameters can be identified without an exclusion restriction. We also propose two-step estimation procedures based on nonlinear least squares and Poisson pseudo-maximum likelihood. To the best of our knowledge, this paper is the first to apply PPML to a Poisson regression model with sample selection. The consistency of both estimators is established, and a robust two-step sandwich covariance matrix is proposed to account for the estimation error from the first-step selection model.
  In addition, factorial moments are used to recover the variance of the latent outcome error and the correlations between the outcome and selection errors. Monte Carlo simulations show that ignoring sample selection leads to persistent bias when the outcome and selection errors are correlated, while the proposed PPML estimator substantially reduces this bias and is more stable than nonlinear least squares, especially under moderate and strong selection dependence.

</details>


### [90] [Summary Indices in Treatment Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21393)
*Danil Fedchenko*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究将多个结果合并为汇总指数以估计因果效应的做法，推导隐含权重，揭示权重可为负且无界，导致指数效应可能与所有分量效应符号相反，并提出两种有效推断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汇总指数被广泛用于因果效应估计，但其隐含权重很少被报告，且常被认为能提高统计功效，这些做法的有效性缺乏严格检验。

Method: 对于常见估计量和指数构造，推导指数效应估计的隐含权重；针对逆协方差加权指数分析权重性质；提出考虑数据依赖权重的方差估计量和无需校正的平移t检验。

Result: 逆协方差加权指数的权重可为负且幅度无界，指数效应可能与每个分量效应符号相反；常规无效应零假设的t检验仍有效；汇总指数通常并不能提高统计功效。

Conclusion: 汇总指数的解释需要谨慎，应报告隐含权重；研究提供了有效的推断方法，并通过三个已发表研究进行了说明。

Abstract: This paper studies the practice of combining multiple outcomes into a summary index to estimate a causal effect. For common estimators and index constructions, the estimate equals a weighted sum of the estimated effects on the components, with weights that are implicit and rarely reported. The paper derives the weights and shows that, for inverse-covariance-weighted indices, they can be negative and unrestricted in magnitude, so the index effect can have the opposite sign to every component effect. The paper proposes two procedures for valid inference on the index effect: a variance estimator that accounts for the data-dependent weights, and a shifted t-test that requires no such correction. Conventional t-tests of the null of no effect remain valid. Contrary to common claims, summary indices do not generally improve power. Three published studies illustrate the results.

</details>


### [91] [The "Rough" HAR Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21587)
*Mikkel Bennedsen,Kim Christensen,Peter Korsbakke Christensen,Jun Yu,Chen Zhang*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出用带MA(1)项的离散AR/HAR模型近似粗糙波动率连续时间模型，发现MA系数与Hurst参数几乎线性相关，实证表明"rough"模型预测更优且更易估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙波动率连续时间模型（由分数布朗噪声驱动）虽表现优异，但估计复杂、需专门软件，难以广泛应用。作者希望找到一种离散时间近似方法，使其既保留粗糙性特征，又能用标准工具估计。

Method: 从理论上证明：当Hurst参数H<0.5时，分数高斯噪声的Wold表示将依赖集中在第一滞后项，因此可用MA(1)分量近似粗糙性；对AR和HAR模型加入MA(1)，构成"rough" AR与"rough" HAR模型，并考察MA系数与Hurst参数的映射关系。

Result: 在十只ETF的对数RV上估计，所有资产均出现显著负MA系数，隐含Hurst参数与连续时间模型估计高度一致；样本外预测中，"rough"模型在几乎所有资产和期限上优于经典模型，短期收益最大，精度与粗糙连续时间模型相当。

Conclusion: 负MA(1)分量是HAR文献中一个被长期忽视的重要特征；"rough"模型以极低估计成本实现了与粗糙连续时间模型相当的预测精度。

Abstract: This paper proposes discrete-time approximations to rough continuous-time models of realized variance (RV). The leading rough models can be viewed as autoregressive processes driven by fractional Gaussian noise. We show that the Wold representation of this noise concentrates its dependence at the first lag when the Hurst parameter is below one half. Augmenting the autoregressive (AR) and heterogeneous autoregressive (HAR) models with a first-order moving-average (MA(1)) component therefore approximates the roughness, and the MA coefficient maps almost linearly into the Hurst parameter. We refer to these extensions as the "rough" AR and "rough" HAR models. Estimating them on the log RV of ten ETFs, we find negative MA coefficients for every asset, and the implied Hurst parameters align closely with the estimates from the continuous-time models. In the HAR literature, the negative MA(1) component is a significant feature that has been largely overlooked. In out-of-sample comparisons, the "rough" models outperform their classical counterparts for nearly every asset and horizon, with the largest gains at short horizons, and their accuracy is comparable to that of the rough continuous-time models but much easier to estimate by standard off-the-shelf software.

</details>


### [92] [Match forecasts in UEFA club competitions: Elo ratings versus Transfermarkt valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21674)
*Gergely Csurilla,László Csató*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文比较了俱乐部Elo评分与Transfermarkt球队身价两种赛前实力指标，通过预测2020/21至2024/25赛季欧冠和欧联杯比赛结果来评估其预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中常用两种代理指标衡量欧洲足球俱乐部的赛前实力：基于表现的Football Club Elo Ratings和基于群体估计的Transfermarkt身价。但二者预测能力孰优孰劣尚缺乏系统比较。

Method: 以2020/21至2024/25赛季欧冠和欧联杯比赛为样本，分别及组合使用Elo评分与Transfermarkt身价，评估其对比赛结果的样本外预测精度，并比较不同的聚合方式。

Result: 两种指标单独使用时预测精度几乎相同；组合使用仅带来小幅提升，且最优聚合方式对预测目标敏感。

Conclusion: 基于Elo评分的种子排位（几乎）是最优的。

Abstract: The pre-season strengths of European football clubs are usually measured by two proxies in the literature. Football Club Elo Ratings provide strictly performance-based Elo ratings from the early days of the European Cups, while Transfermarkt valuations are crowd-based estimates of squad market values. This paper compares them by evaluating their ability to forecast the results of matches played in the UEFA Champions League and the UEFA Europa League between the seasons 2020/21 and 2024/25. The two indicators yield almost identical out-of-sample accuracy when used separately. Combining the two measures leads to a modest improvement, but the best aggregation procedure is sensitive to the forecast target. Our results suggest that seeding based on Elo ratings would be (closely) optimal.

</details>

### [93] [Adapting the Actor Model of Concurrency for High-Frequency Trading: Synchronous Message Delivery (fast_send) and a Tick-to-Book Latency Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21173)
*Vincent Maciejewski*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文反驳"actor模型不适合高频交易"的观点，提出并实现了面向同址actor的C++20框架kaspar-hft，通过四项扩展将同步往返降至数十纳秒，实测框架开销低于7微秒解码账面底线的1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: actor模型具备状态隔离、无数据竞争、无死锁和单消息顺序推理等优点，但长期被认为不适合HFT——因其隐含多线程、每actor邮箱、堆分配消息和每次交互的上下文切换，与微秒级预算不兼容。作者认为对同址actor而言这一否定是错误的。

Method: 提出kaspar-hft开源C++20框架，含四项扩展：(1) fast_send——同步投递，发送线程内联执行接收者handler并直接返回回复，且对handler透明；(2) actor groups——多个actor在同一线程上被共享邮箱协同调度；(3) 每actor可选邮箱队列；(4) 内存池。分组同步链在单线程运行，将上下文切换从O(N)降至O(1)，并用线程局部调用链检测在加锁前捕获循环调用。

Result: 微基准显示同步往返为数十纳秒。在实时CME行情（ES、NQ、ZN期货）上，socket到账面延迟由约7微秒的解码与记账底线加每消息斜率构成，框架自身贡献不足底线的1%。尾部延迟由市场非泊松聚集到达过程决定而非actor机制。共享队列group还实现了生产/仿真二元性：同一actor代码可原样用于实盘和确定性回测。

Conclusion: 对于同址actor，actor模型可满足HFT微秒级要求，其开销相对市场数据处理底线可忽略；框架瓶颈不在actor机制而在市场到达过程本身，且该设计统一了实盘与回测代码。

Abstract: The actor model - state isolation, data-race freedom, deadlock resistance, and sequential single-message reasoning - has long been dismissed as unsuitable for high-frequency trading (HFT): actors seem to imply many threads, a mailbox per actor, and a heap-allocated message plus a context switch per interaction, overhead incompatible with a microsecond budget. This paper argues the dismissal is wrong for co-located actors, and supports it both analytically and with a deployed, measured implementation: kaspar-hft, an open-source C++20 framework. Four extensions adapt the model for HFT: fast_send, a synchronous delivery mechanism in which the sending thread runs the receiver's handler inline and returns the reply as a value; actor groups, which co-schedule actors on one thread behind a shared mailbox; per-actor selectable mailbox queues; and a memory pool. fast_send has receiver transparency: the handler cannot tell whether delivery was synchronous or asynchronous, or which thread runs it. A grouped synchronous chain runs on one thread, cutting scheduler context switches from O(N) to O(1), and a thread-local call-chain test catches cyclic invocation before any lock is taken. Microbenchmarks put the synchronous round trip at tens of nanoseconds. On a live CME market-data feed (ES, NQ, ZN futures), socket-to-book latency decomposes into a ~7 microsecond decode-and-book floor plus a per-message slope; the framework's own contribution is under 1% of the floor. The tail is set not by the actor machinery but by the market's non-Poisson, clustered arrival process, characterized in a companion paper. The shared-queue group also yields a production/simulation duality: the same actor code runs unchanged in live trading and deterministic backtest.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RBS-Attention，一种免训练的稀疏预填充方法，通过双分支选择解决块质心掩盖关键 token 的"均值稀释"问题，在保持精度的同时大幅加速长上下文推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理受预填充阶段制约，稠密自注意力需处理整个提示。稀疏块选择可降低成本，但块质心可能掩盖块内高度相关的 token（均值稀释失败模式）。

Method: RBS-Attention 含两个互补选择分支：质心基础分支捕捉平均相关性；救援分支利用最大键块半径及其随提示、层、头变化的分布，识别可能被低估的块。两分支独立阈值化并合并掩码，控制救援块贡献，同时保持常规块稀疏 FlashAttention 执行。

Result: H100 上：独立预填充注意力加速 20.65×，vLLM 预填充注意力加速 11.92×，128K 下 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8 端到端 TTFT 加速 5.97×。稠密 Qwen3-32B 上 RULER 准确率 88.65，对比稠密注意力 89.52；另在 LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench、Video-MME 上评估质量，并测量实际保留率、匹配密度下选择器对比，及块大小、阈值、内存行为。

Conclusion: 半径自适应双分支选择是长上下文预填充的有效方法。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [95] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Attention-Aware Routing (AAR)，利用滑动窗口注意力权重的时域与频谱特征增强MoE路由器，在冻结主干下仅训练路由参数，提升推理并揭示路由-注意力耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由通常仅依据token隐藏状态、上下文信息有限，作者希望利用注意力中蕴含的上下文状态来改进路由，并单独研究路由的作用。

Method: AAR从注意力权重滑动窗口中提取时序与频谱特征（与隐藏状态解耦）来增强路由器；保持基础Transformer冻结，只训练路由参数。

Result: 在OLMoE上GSM8K较仅路由SFT基线提升+3.37pp；路由变化经残差流放大下一层注意力汇，减少长发散生成（错误答案变短、正确答案长度不变）；对深度敏感，浅层应用可能损害事实检索，深层应用提升数学推理。

Conclusion: 路由与注意力构成耦合电路，AAR具深度敏感性，暴露检索-推理张力，使层选择性AAR成为探测各层路由相关信息的受控工具。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [96] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Causal Latent Revision (CaLR)框架，将推理重构为约束潜空间优化，结合AR与扩散模型的优势，在复杂基准上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型存在局部贪婪问题，而扩散语言模型缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构，两者各有优劣，需要结合双方优势并克服缺陷。

Method: 从专家模型采用因果拓扑矩阵（CTM），并结合隐式微分，执行梯度引导的"思维修正"以强制逻辑一致性，从而在并行生成过程中动态自我纠正中间步骤。

Result: 在复杂基准上达到SOTA的扩散语言模型性能，超越强自回归基线，并在数独等约束任务中展现更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaLR通过约束潜空间优化有效统一了因果结构与并行生成，证明了结合AR与扩散优势的可行性。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [97] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段参数高效框架，将DINOv3 ViT适配于合成孔径声呐自动目标识别，发现仅LoRA微调即为充分手段，后续的硬负样本挖掘与监督对比学习并无显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAS自动目标识别对海军能力重要，但深度学习受制于目标图像稀缺、背景杂波强以及需要人工介入评估等问题，因此需要一种参数高效的适配方案来弥合自然图像预训练与水下声学传播之间的差距。

Method: 采用三阶段参数高效框架适配DINOv3 ViT：第一阶段冻结主干并使用LoRA微调；第二阶段用硬负样本挖掘强化对岩石、沉积物等声学伪目标的决策边界；第三阶段用监督对比学习（SupCon）分离目标与杂波表征。在海上SAS数据上采用任务级地理划分评估，所有对比在85%测试召回率下进行，并重复三次随机种子。

Result: LoRA是主要效果来源，使AUPRC从0.300提升至0.679±0.027（同一冻结主干），Rank 4仅训练0.26%的权重即可达到该结果。两个精修阶段均未超过匹配对照组：硬负样本挖掘相比等量随机课程AUPRC变化为-0.0045±0.0119；SupCon相比前一阶段变化为+0.0002±0.0096。

Conclusion: 精修阶段无增益的零结果表明，挖掘发生编码器已拟合的数据上，且监督阶段已施加了大部分目标-杂波几何结构。因此一个高效适配阶段即足够，堆叠式精修并非必要。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [98] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过分析注意力图中的信息流拓扑（Forman-Ricci曲率）来区分LLM的幻觉与非幻觉响应，提出一种单次推理的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉检测需要有效区分幻觉与非幻觉响应。现有方法多依赖注意力特征或多响应基线，但缺乏对注意力图中信息流拓扑结构的刻画，忽略信息瓶颈等结构性线索。

Method: 分析注意力图的Forman-Ricci曲率以识别信息瓶颈的结构模式；提出一种捕捉注意力头半局部与全局信息流特征的检测方法，采用单次推理（single-pass）进行检测。

Result: 在多个LLM与两个幻觉检测基准上，单次推理方法一致优于现有基于注意力和多响应的基线，并在多种LLM架构上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 因果生成过程中token间上下文共享受损与幻觉发生强相关；幻觉响应表现为过度依赖自注意力、上下文检索弥散或信息过度压缩（over-squashing），尤其在最后一层transformer中。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [99] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究微调如何改变LLM内部表示（注意力模式和逐层激活），并探讨这些变化与EAP识别出的任务相关组件的关系。结果发现EAP组件集中于特定层，其分布与表示变化最大的层基本无关，且跨任务EAP组件重叠并不意味着性能迁移，反而可能导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调虽被广泛用于将LLM适配到下游任务，但其如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏理解，因此需要探究微调对内部表示的影响及其与任务性能相关组件的联系。

Method: 通过分析微调前后LLM的注意力模式和逐层激活，考察内部表示的变化；并使用EAP识别驱动任务性能的关键组件（如注意力头和logit级激活），比较其层级分布与表示变化层的关系，以及跨任务的组件重叠与性能迁移情况。

Result: EAP识别的组件集中在特定层，表明存在一定的功能局部化；这些组件的层级分布与表示变化最大的层基本不相关；跨任务EAP组件重叠并不能带来性能迁移（尤其当任务性质不同，如分类与生成），甚至当两个任务EAP组件高度重叠时，在一个任务上微调会导致另一任务性能下降。

Conclusion: 微调对LLM内部表示的重塑与EAP识别的任务相关组件在层级上并不一致，且组件重叠不保证跨任务正迁移，反而可能引发任务间干扰，提示微调需关注任务间组件冲突。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [100] [AI-GRACE: A Use-Case Operationalization Framework for Agentic AI: From Organizational Objectives and Obligations to Deployment Capabilities and Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21192)
*John Cuneo,David Chun,Gaurav Khanna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI-GRACE 框架，将组织治理与技术实现对接，用于 agentic AI 用例的治理、风险、保证、控制与证据落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织部署 agentic AI 时，不仅要评估模型是否可信，还需明确用例应验证、控制和观测什么，以达成目标并满足合规义务。

Method: 基于专业观察与标准/文献的定向综合，采用设计科学定位方法贡献，并以情境方法工程指导情境化裁剪与复用。

Result: 框架先确定目标与义务，再在七个风险域（含使命与价值实现）评估风险，推导部署前保证、运行时控制与证据需求，指导能力资格、差距评估和逻辑架构；Agent Operating Envelope 规定许可动作与升级条件，RAIL 概括授权自主性；并以虚构零售银行应用示例说明。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为组织判断“必须实现什么、已支持什么、仍未解决什么”提供可追溯依据；其是否改善部署决策、效率与复用仍需实证评估。

Abstract: Organizations deploying agentic artificial intelligence must determine more than whether a model is trustworthy; they must establish what to validate, control, and observe for a use case to deliver its intended outcome while meeting applicable obligations. This paper proposes AI-GRACE (Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence) as a use-case operationalization framework connecting organizational governance with technical implementation. The proposal draws on professional observations and a purposive synthesis of standards and literature, using design science to frame the method contribution and situational method engineering to guide contextual tailoring and reuse. The framework establishes objectives and obligations and then assesses risks in seven proposed domains, including mission and value realization. It derives requirements for assurance before deployment, runtime controls, and evidence, which guide capability qualification, gap assessment, and a logical architecture. An Agent Operating Envelope specifies permitted actions and escalation conditions, while Risk-Aligned Independence Levels (RAIL) summarize the authorized independence. A fictional retail banking application illustrates the method. The contribution is a traceable basis for deciding what an organization must implement, what it already supports, and what remains unresolved. Empirical evaluation must establish whether it improves deployment decisions, efficiency, and reuse.

</details>


### [101] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TinyCeNN-LM框架，通过质量门控的后训练转换，用类CeNN层替换预训练语言模型中的注意力层，强调保守验证而非通用加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替换预训练语言模型中的注意力机制是一个兼容性问题：看似合理的替代层可能改变后续层所期望的表示，因此需要谨慎、可验证的转换方法。

Method: 提出质量门控后训练转换框架，采用CeNN启发的细胞循环层（有界局部处理、紧凑循环记忆、路由、融合及接受或回滚验证）。研究三种实现：Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion、PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32。严格PDelta3转换仅在表示和NLL指标通过固定阈值时接受该层。

Result: 在SmolLM2-135M上，第0-2层被接受，累积ΔNLL=+0.01209；第3层因表示保真度不达标被拒绝（NLL尚可）。在Qwen3.5-0.8B上，全注意力层3、7、11被接受，最终ΔNLL=+0.02073。Integrated Memory将困惑度控制在-0.07%至+0.93%内，总缓存减少最多6.01%。200项下游抽检显示转换后Qwen准确率为28.5%–32.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用保守的、质量门控的结构转换，而非通用的注意力替换或加速。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [102] [Reducing Barriers to Academic Support: Evaluating a Course-Specific RAG System for Addressing Help-Seeking Disparities in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21600)
*Andy Gray,Jake Hobbs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍并评估 Beacon——一个面向课程的 RAG 系统，提供私密、即时、与模块对齐的学术支持，帮助学生降低求助门槛并促进独立学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生获取学术支持存在不平等：有人主动求助，有人因焦虑、怕被评判、期望不明或信心不足而犹豫；在编程等累积性、认知负荷高的计算教育中尤为突出。通用生成式 AI 虽被广泛使用，但回答可能不准确、缺乏情境或与课程期望不符。

Method: 采用设计型研究（DBR）迭代开发 Beacon，基于经批准的课程教学材料进行检索增强生成；通过混合方法评估，结合问卷与半结构化访谈，覆盖高校学生与教职员工。

Result: 学生认为 Beacon 的回答与模块内容高度契合，比不受限的生成式 AI 更可信；青睐其使用伪代码和支架式解释而非直接给答案。尽管仍对未经核实就信任 AI 持谨慎态度，但视其为咨询讲师或官方资源前有价值的第一支持点。

Conclusion: 精心设计的课程专属 AI 系统可居于自主学习与正式支持之间，降低学术支持门槛；教育 AI 不应取代教师，而在扩大指导可及性的同时保留讲师的教学角色时最具价值。

Abstract: Access to academic support is a key determinant of student success, yet students experience it unequally: some readily seek help from lecturers or tutors, while others hesitate due to anxiety, fear of judgement, uncertainty about expectations, or low confidence in their understanding. This may be especially evident in computing education, where programming tasks are cumulative and cognitively demanding. Although students increasingly turn to general-purpose generative AI tools, these can produce responses that are inaccurate, insufficiently contextualised, or misaligned with module expectations. This study presents and evaluates Beacon, a course-specific Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system providing private, immediate, module-aligned academic support. Grounding responses in approved teaching materials, Beacon was designed to lower barriers to help-seeking while encouraging independent learning. Using a design-based research approach, Beacon was developed iteratively and evaluated via mixed methods, combining questionnaires and semi-structured interviews with students and staff at a Higher Education institution. Students described Beacon's responses as closely aligned with module content and more trustworthy than unrestricted generative AI tools, valuing its use of pseudocode and scaffolded explanations over direct solutions. Although participants remained cautious about trusting AI-generated responses without verification, they viewed the system as a valuable first point of support before consulting lecturers or official resources. The findings suggest that carefully designed course-specific AI systems may reduce barriers to academic support by occupying an intermediary space between independent study and formal support. Rather than replacing educators, educational AI may be most valuable when it broadens access to guidance while preserving the pedagogical role of lecturers.

</details>


### [103] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InterviewPlayground——一个基于记忆增强患者模拟器的临床评估平台,用于对AI精神科问诊系统进行质量保证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者使用AI辅助精神科问诊系统前,医疗系统需要实用的方法来按临床质量标准常规评估这些工具;现有评估需支持不同问诊风格比较、降低临床医生负担并衡量临床相关表现。

Method: 基于记忆增强患者模拟器构建开放式AI访谈评估平台InterviewPlayground;使用专家撰写的病例创建交互式患者,搭建模拟问诊平台,并设计相关评估模式。6名临床医生进行25分钟评估,与基于GPT的LLM问诊者对比。

Result: LLM恢复更多临床相关条目(88.0% vs. 38.9%),但做出更多非基于访谈的临床推断(56.8% vs. 27.8%),且较少刻画已识别的安全顾虑(33.3% vs. 66.7%)。

Conclusion: 该平台为AI精神科问诊任务的部署质量保证奠定基础。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake. In a pilot of 6 clinicians in a 25-minute assessment compared to a GPT-based LLM intake interviewer, the LLM recovered more of the clinically relevant items embedded in the patient vignettes (88.0% vs. 38.9%), but made more clinical inferences not based on the interview (56.8% vs. 27.8%), and characterized identified safety concerns less often (33.3% vs. 66.7%), setting the stage for deployed quality assurance for this task.

</details>


### [104] [Can Agents Design Better Chips with a Higher Level Abstraction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21157)
*Zijian Ding,Yang Zou,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索LLM代理如何通过更高层次抽象设计更优芯片，提出将基于代理的HLS设计（AHRR）与HLS后RTL精炼相结合的工作流，在11项任务基准上相比直接RTL设计取得2.6倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理芯片设计方法大多直接在RTL层面操作，作者希望探究代理是否能借助更高层次抽象来设计更好的芯片。

Method: 对比四种流程：直接RTL设计、基于代理的HLS设计、编译器后HLS精炼、HLS后RTL精炼；并将基于代理的HLS设计与HLS后RTL精炼组合为AHRR。以FPGA作为端到端评估平台，但指出设计流程权衡基本与目标技术无关。

Result: 在包含11项任务的多样化基准套件上，AHRR相比直接RTL设计实现2.6倍几何平均加速。案例研究表明HLS将设计知识蒸馏为代理可利用的抽象，而RTL精炼可恢复底层优化机会。

Conclusion: AHRR是面向代理式芯片设计的有前景工作流，代码与评估产物已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for chip design, but most existing approaches operate directly at RTL. We ask whether agents can design better chips by leveraging higher-level abstractions. We compare Direct RTL Design, Agent-based HLS Design, Post-Compiler HLS Refinement, and Post-HLS RTL Refinement, and combine Agent-based HLS Design with Post-HLS RTL Refinement as Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement (AHRR). We use FPGAs as a practical, easy-to-deploy platform for end-to-end evaluation, but note that the design-flow tradeoffs we study are largely independent of the target technology. Across a diverse 11-tasks benchmark suite, AHRR achieves a 2.6$\times$ geometric-mean speedup over Direct RTL Design across our benchmark suite. Case studies show that HLS distills design knowledge into abstractions that agents can leverage, while RTL refinement recovers lower-level optimization opportunities. Together, these results make AHRR a promising workflow for agentic chip design. The code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/ZijD/AHRR.

</details>


### [105] [SpecOpt: Contact-Diff Reasoning for Agentic Molecule Optimization Toward Binding Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21165)
*Thao Nguyen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecOpt任务与agentic框架，通过残基感知的差异化对接信息让LLM修改现有药物结构，提升对靶点相对脱靶的选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脱靶结合是小分子药物副作用的主要来源，但现有基于结构的分子设计方法多关注从头生成选择性化合物，而非改善已有、表征充分的药物的选择性。

Method: 定义SpecOpt任务：在保持结构相似性和成药性的约束下，对现有化合物做结构修饰以增强对目标靶点相对已知脱靶的结合偏好。基于ChEMBL构建基准（用药理机制注释定目标靶点，用实测活性定脱靶），并开发智能体框架：将化合物对接至目标与脱靶，用残基感知的原子-蛋白接触比较构象，把差异化相互作用交给大语言模型提出修饰方案，仅保留满足分子相似性、ADMET及对接选择性标准的候选。

Result: 在915个化合物上，智能体为84.8%的化合物扩大了目标-脱靶结合差距，平均差距从-0.72提升至+0.47 kcal/mol，与起始化合物的平均Tanimoto相似度为0.72。消融实验中，将残基身份替换为二元接触指标后，全部29个化合物均无改善，表明残基特异性接触信息是关键优化信号。

Conclusion: SpecOpt确立了独立的分子设计问题，并证明残基感知的差异化相互作用是提升现有化合物特异性的有效信号。

Abstract: Off-target protein binding is a major source of adverse effects for small-molecule drugs, yet most structure-based molecular design methods focus on generating selective compounds de novo rather than improving the selectivity of existing, well- characterized drugs. We introduce specificity optimization (SpecOpt), a molecular design task that seeks constrained structural modifications to an existing compound that increase its binding preference for an intended target over known off-targets while preserving its structural identity and drug-like properties. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct a ChEMBL-derived benchmark from compound-target interaction data, identifying intended targets through curated drug-mechanism annotations and off- targets through measured activities. We then develop an agentic framework that docks each compound against its intended target and off-targets, compares the resulting poses through residue-aware atom-protein contacts, and provides these differential interactions to a large language model to propose targeted structural modifications. Candidates are retained only if they satisfy molecular similarity, ADMET, and target-off-target docking selectivity criteria. On 915 compounds, the agent improves the target- off-target binding gap for 84.8% of compounds, shifting the mean gap from -0.72 to +0.47 kcal/mol while maintaining a mean Tanimoto similarity of 0.72 to the starting compounds. Ablation studies identify residue-specific contact information as the critical optimization signal: replacing residue identities with binary contact indicators eliminates improvement on all 29 ablation compounds. These results establish SpecOpt as a distinct molecular design problem and demonstrate residue-aware differential interactions as an effective signal for improving the specificity of existing compounds.

</details>


### [106] [Implicit Rule Induction with Test-Time Task Embeddings in ARC-like Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21181)
*Adrien Deliège,Claas Beger,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Melanie Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出两步测试时训练协议Embed-TTT，先微调任务嵌入再微调骨干网络，使嵌入更好地对齐真实规则，并区分ARC类任务中规则归纳与规则执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC等基准无法判断模型成功是推断出真实规则还是依赖捷径，需要更清晰地分离规则归纳与规则执行。

Method: 在VARC框架下引入两步TTT：先仅微调表示变换规则的任务嵌入（Embed-TTT），再冻结嵌入微调预训练骨干网络。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC及两个已知规则数据集上，Embed-TTT产生更优任务嵌入，提升规则对齐、检索与线性探测准确率；仅优化<0.01%参数即可解决部分任务，完整两步流程进一步提升性能；可恢复参数规则的几何结构，支持规则插值但不支持外推。

Conclusion: 研究支持在ARC类评估中更清晰地区分规则归纳与规则执行，并推动构建能区分分布内与分布外规则的基准。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus and related benchmarks evaluate whether AI models can solve novel reasoning tasks, but often leave unclear whether success reflects inference of the intended underlying rule or reliance on shortcuts. We address this gap by studying test-time task embeddings in Vision ARC (VARC), a model in which a pre-trained backbone is complemented by a trainable embedding representing the transformation rule. In the original VARC, test-time training (TTT) is jointly applied to the backbone and task embedding. Here we introduce a novel two-step TTT protocol: first finetune only the task embedding (Embed-TTT), then freeze it and finetune the backbone. Across ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and two controlled datasets with known rules, Embed-TTT consistently yields improved task embeddings, ones that align better with underlying task rules, improve embedding-based retrieval, and enable accurate linear probing of known rules. Qualitatively, Embed-TTT identifies more semantically meaningful relations between test and train tasks on ARC-AGI-1. We also show that optimizing only task embeddings (less than 0.01% of model parameters) already solves a non-trivial fraction of ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and Mini-ARC tasks, while the full two-step pipeline improves final performance. Finally, we show that Embed-TTT recovers the underlying geometric structure of parametric rules and learns compositional capabilities that enable rule-wise interpolation, but not extrapolation. These findings support a clearer separation between rule induction and rule execution in ARC-like evaluations, motivating benchmarks that better distinguish in-distribution from out-of-distribution rules.

</details>


### [107] [Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21208)
*Ana Nunez,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoVer是一个自博弈GRPO框架，通过信息增益奖励和三阶段多样性选择解决自训代码生成中的宽松性崩溃与集中性偏差问题，在多个基准上提升pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈方法让单一语言模型同时充当代码编写者和测试作者，有望突破固定测试套件限制，但存在两个问题：宽松性崩溃（pass率奖励被琐碎、无区分度的测试最大化）和集中性偏差（i.i.d.采样测试聚集于模态输入、增大估计方差）。

Method: 提出CoVer单策略GRPO框架。其一，用信息增益奖励，以自生成测试的pass/fail向量与有真值锚定的分级正确性信号y∈[0,1]^m间的互信息评分，并以协方差符号门控，仅奖励正向有区分度的测试。其二，三阶段多样性感知选择（无效性、输入字符串、执行profile过滤）将候选池剪枝为行为非冗余套件，在固定执行预算下提升IG估计的有效样本量。

Result: 在LiveBench、MBPP、LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、CodeForces五个基准上，基于Qwen2.5-Instruct，7B提升one-shot pass@1 +5.8分，14B提升+7.1分，两个规模均取得最高宏平均。作为CodeT排序流程的即插即用backbone，CoVer-7B额外提升+3.5分。

Conclusion: CoVer证明协同训练对生成与选择具有双重收益，有效缓解自博弈测试生成的宽松性崩溃与集中性偏差。

Abstract: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.

</details>


### [108] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EnterpriseVal——一个用例级的企业级 GenAI 评估系统，弥合公共基准（"模型能做什么"）与部署决策（"此工作流是否适合、可靠、安全、值得扩展"）之间的测量鸿沟，并通过全球银行三项工作流的试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型已能产出专家级交付物，但多数企业 GenAI 项目无法体现可衡量的业务效果，大量智能体项目面临取消。作者认为这本质是测量问题：公共基准回答"模型能做什么"，而部署决策需要回答"在此处、用我们的数据、在我们的管控下，该工作流是否适配、可靠、安全、值得规模化"。

Method: EnterpriseVal 是一个用例级评估系统，包含五部分：(i) 对用例及冻结的社会技术配置（模型、提示、检索、工具、护栏、人工监督）的形式化规范，配合自主性等级与后果层级共同决定评估强度；(ii) 覆盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保证与监督的指标目录；(iii) 通过预测驱动推断（PPI），将盲审专家判断与校准的 LLM-as-judge 评分相结合的评分协议；(iv) 以可执行算法表述的双层阈值门，将带置信边界的指标向量映射为 REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE 决策；(v) 将评审者捕获率作为测量参数的价值-风险模型。

Result: 在全球银行三项工作流的试点中：信贷备忘录撰写任务，最佳模型的人工评分引用精确率达 88%、幻觉率 1.6%，超过 70% 和 5% 的门槛；流程转换任务中，分析师精修工作量从估计的每份文档 27.4 小时降至 2.9 小时。
