This perspective clarifies what current methods estimate and control, what remains uncontrolled, and why reliable validation, targeted recovery, calibrated uncertainty, and matched-budget evaluation are central open problems.

</details>


### [145] [A Smoothed Discrepancy Principle for Random Feature Methods and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21017)
*Mike Nguyen,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于差异原则的多尺度早停规则，用于谱正则化方法，实现全平滑度自适应，并通过随机特征近似降低计算成本、保持最优统计保证，进而为神经网络宽度选择提供数据驱动建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非参数回归中，谱正则化方法的早停选择往往无法在所有平滑度水平上实现完全自适应，且在大数据集上计算成本高，缺乏数据驱动的超参数（如随机特征数、网络宽度）选择依据。

Method: 基于差异原则，提出适用于一般核估计器的多尺度停止规则；进一步引入基于随机特征近似（random feature approximation）的扩展，将随机特征与神经正切核（NTK）情形下的神经网络建立联系，从而联合选择最优停止时间与随机特征数量（网络宽度）。

Result: 该多尺度规则在well-specified情形下对所有平滑度水平实现完全自适应；随机特征扩展在降低大数据计算成本的同时保持极小极大最优统计保证；并证明同时选择的网络宽度与停止时间可使神经网络在无需已知平滑度或容量参数的情况下达到极小极大最优学习率。

Conclusion: 该方法为谱正则化的早停与随机特征/神经网络宽度选择提供了统一、数据驱动且理论最优的框架。

Abstract: We study data-driven early stopping for spectral regularisation methods in the classical non-parametric regression setting. Building on the discrepancy principle, we propose a multi-scale stopping rule that applies to general kernel estimators and show that, unlike previous approaches, it achieves full adaptivity over all smoothness levels in the well-specified case. A key contribution of our work is an extension based on random feature approximations, which reduces computational cost on large datasets while preserving minimax-optimal statistical guarantees. Our procedure not only selects an optimal stopping time but also provides a fully data-driven choice of the number of random features needed to achieve optimal rates. Through the established connection between random features and neural networks in the neural tangent kernel regime, our method further yields a principled, data-driven recommendation for the network width. We prove that the resulting simultaneously chosen width and stopping time allow neural networks to attain minimax-optimal learning rates without prior knowledge of smoothness or capacity parameters.

</details>


### [146] [Triply-Scalable Equivariant Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21085)
*Tim Steinert,David Ginsbourger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立高斯过程等变性与条件化的理论结果，构造等变稀疏GP与"三重可扩展"等变GP，在SO(2)等变向量场及分子偶极矩预测上实现准确且带不确定性的高效预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP能提供原理性的概率预测并编码先验（含等变性），但在大规模科学问题中受计算成本限制；等变神经网络虽常用却缺乏GP的不确定性量化，而分子研究等应用非常需要该能力。高维输入与大对称群进一步要求可扩展性。

Method: 分析GP等变性与条件化的相互作用，借助合适的均值函数与协方差核获得等变稀疏GP；实例化一类灵活的无积分等变核，提出三重可扩展等变GP。用于SO(2)等变向量场与分子性质预测，并开发无矩阵等变全GP实现（精确Kronecker降维+预条件共轭梯度），比较不同诱导点选择方法。

Result: 合成SO(2)等变场实验及基于量子化学模拟的N-甲基甲酰胺电偶极矩预测中，方法取得准确、带不确定性感知的预测，计算成本仅为经典GP推理的一小部分。

Conclusion: 等变稀疏GP框架在保持等变性与不确定性量化的同时实现可扩展与数据高效，适合大规模科学问题中的等变建模。

Abstract: Gaussian processes (GPs) provide principled probabilistic predictions while encoding prior knowledge, including equivariances. Yet, their use in large-scale scientific problems is limited by computational cost. Equivariant neural networks are common but typically lack the uncertainty quantification offered by GPs, which is valuable in applications such as molecular research. High-dimensional inputs and large symmetry groups further demand scalability. We establish results pertaining to the interplay of GP equivariance and conditioning and leverage them to obtain equivariant sparse GPs through suitable mean functions and covariance kernels. We instantiate this framework with a flexible class of integration-free equivariant kernels, yielding scalable and data-efficient GP inference. In particular, we introduce triply scalable equivariant Gaussian processes.
  We employ equivariant sparse variational Gaussian processes for $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant vector fields and molecular property prediction. Alongside the SVGP, we develop a matrix-free equivariant full-GP implementation that combines an exact Kronecker reduction with preconditioned conjugate-gradient solves, enabling fast and scalable evaluation of the full joint predictive density. We further compare different approaches for selecting inducing points in the equivariant sparse GP models. Our test cases include synthetic $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant fields as well as the prediction of electric dipole moments of N-methylformamide based on quantum chemistry simulations, achieving accurate, uncertainty-aware predictions at a fraction of the computational cost of classical GP inference.

</details>


### [147] [Diagonalized Attention for Individualized Regression: Latent-Row Localization and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21320)
*Borui Peng,Liwei Lin,Feifei Wang,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出针对矩阵值协变量的个体化稀疏回归框架，用对角化注意力机制识别每个样本自身的信号行，并给出存在性理论和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文本/图像表示是矩阵值（行对应token、patch等局部特征），预测信息稀疏且随样本而异，导致经典共享支撑的稀疏回归不适用。

Method: 构建个体化稀疏回归框架：每个观测有自身的感兴趣行，但回归效应在总体间共享；用query-key打分定位样本特定信号行，用value矩阵做回归，参数维度与样本量无关。

Result: 证明在分数分离与集中条件下，单头与多头对角化注意力能以高概率恢复潜在行并给出预测风险界；模拟在回归与误设分类中表现良好；真实情感分析提升准确率并可解释地选择token。

Conclusion: 该理论为注意力打分如何定位异质矩阵数据中的样本特定信号提供了统计学解释，方法能泛化到新样本且不依赖其响应。

Abstract: Modern text and image representations are often matrix-valued, with rows corresponding to tokens, patches, or other local feature vectors. Predictive information is often sparse but sample-specific, making classical sparse regression methods with a common support poorly suited to this heterogeneity. This paper formalizes an individualized sparse regression framework for matrix-valued covariates in which each observation has its own rows of interest, while the associated regression effects are shared across the population. To estimate this model, we introduce a diagonalized attention mechanism that uses query--key scores to localize sample-specific signal rows and a value matrix for downstream regression. The proposed method has a parameter dimension independent of sample size and can identify rows of interest for new observations without their responses. We establish existence theorems showing that, under suitable score-separation and concentration conditions, single-head and multi-head diagonalized attention models recover the latent rows with high probability, yielding prediction risk bounds. Our theory therefore provides a statistical explanation of how attention-based scoring localizes sample-specific signals in heterogeneous matrix-valued data. Simulations demonstrate strong prediction and localization in regression and misspecified classification across varying sample sizes, dimensions, and signal cardinalities. Real sentiment analyses show improved classification accuracy and interpretable token selection.

</details>


### [148] [Sparse Identification for Automatic Large-Scale Screening: A Constraint-Aware Framework with Ultra Fast Decoding Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21321)
*Jianing Li,Li Chai,Yingcheng Lai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出LoSc（逻辑筛查）方法，一种超快、准确且具理论基础的群体检测框架，仅用O(klogn)次混合检测即可识别所有阳性样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情初期需通过大规模筛查识别少量感染者，但试剂与检测能力有限。现有群体检测方法存在计算复杂度高或识别精度低的问题，且缺乏针对样本使用约束和稀释效应的稀疏识别严格理论分析。

Method: LoSc引入一种新颖解码算法，采用极简选择策略，仅依赖逻辑操作，可直接硬件实现；显式将稀释效应与样本使用约束纳入混合设计，并建立理论保证以指导最优混合配置。

Result: 仿真验证了LoSc在有效性、效率和可扩展性上的优越表现；理论上可在O(klogn)次混合检测内识别所有阳性。

Conclusion: LoSc为自动大规模筛查提供快速可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In the early stages of a pandemic, identification of a small number of infected individuals through large-scale screening is critical for pandemic control, yet remains challenging under limited reagents and testing capacity. Existing group testing methods suffer from either high computational complexity or low identification accuracy. Even worse, no available methods provide theoretically rigorous analysis for sparse identification with hard constraints caused by the sample usage constraint and the dilution effect existing ubiquitously in practical applications. In this article, we propose the Logic Screening method (LoSc), an ultra fast, accurate, and theoretically grounded framework for large-scale screening. LoSc introduces a novel decoding algorithm with a very simple selection strategy, achieving identification of all positives with only O(klogn) pooled tests. The decoding relies only on logical operations, enabling direct hardware implementation and yielding ultra fast computational implementation. Moreover, LoSc explicitly incorporates dilution and sample usage constraints into pooling designs, and establishes theoretical guarantees to guide optimal pooling configurations. Extensive simulations confirm the superior effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability. We believe LoSc offers a fast and reliable solution for automatic large-scale screening.

</details>


### [149] [Brownian Heads for Deep ReLU Representations: Activation Mass and the Cost of Same-Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21422)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究深度ReLU表示接有界范数预测器（"布朗头"）的条件经验Rademacher复杂度，在固定表示与同一样本选择两种情形下给出精确对偶恒等式与紧界，揭示选择代价与实现规模的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度表示学习常在同一样本上完成隐藏特征选择与预测器拟合，而在选择之后再分析固定特征会忽略选择成本；作者希望刻画这种同一样本选择带来的额外复杂度。

Method: 针对加性或Lévy-Brownian RKHS中的布朗头，先对固定表示导出精确对偶恒等式及基于激活质量与隐藏向量平均范数的紧界；在同一表示选择情形下，将表示上确界化为二次Rademacher过程，并利用布朗层饼与高斯投影恒等式化简为逐坐标或带符号投影阈值迹。对输入两两不同的样本，构造显式标量ReLU族匹配有限迹与VC速率。

Result: 得到固定表示的精确对偶与紧界；同一样本选择下通过层饼/高斯投影恒等式分离实现规模与选择复杂度；显式标量ReLU族在实现迹与包络层面以通用常数匹配有限迹和VC速率；归纳范数收缩给出矩形、秩亏ReLU网络的架构级界。实验验证紧界与速率，展示固定激活质量下的选择差距，并评估布朗头的预测可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为深度ReLU表示在同一样本选择下的泛化复杂度提供了精确工具与紧界，明确了选择代价，并给出架构级结论与实验支持。

Abstract: Deep representation learning often selects hidden features and fits the final predictor on the same sample, so fixed-feature analysis performed after selection can omit selection cost. We study the conditional empirical Rademacher complexity of deep ReLU representations followed by bounded-norm predictors in additive or Lévy-Brownian RKHSs, termed Brownian heads. For a fixed representation, we derive an exact dual identity and sharp bounds in terms of activation mass, the average norm of the observed hidden vectors. Under same-sample selection, the representation supremum induces a quadratic Rademacher process. Brownian layer-cake and Gaussian-projection identities reduce it to coordinatewise or signed projected threshold traces, separating realized scale from selection complexity. For samples with pairwise-distinct inputs, explicit scalar ReLU families match the finite-trace and VC rates up to universal constants at the realized trace-and-envelope level. Induced-norm contraction also yields architecture-level bounds for rectangular, rank-deficient ReLU networks. Experiments verify the sharp bounds and rates, exhibit a selection gap at fixed activation mass, and assess the predictive feasibility of Brownian heads.

</details>


### [150] [Improving the Predictive Performance of Bootstrap Aggregating by Dirichlet Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21454)
*Quoc Viet Le,Joonha Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文基于Breiman关于降低树间相关性可提升随机森林的观察，提出DM与DW两种变体，通过浓度参数α调节样本重加权，并提供理论判据指导轻量调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Breiman曾指出，在不削弱单棵树性能的前提下降低树间相关性可改善随机森林。作者希望在这一原则基础上探索新的随机森林变体。

Method: 提出Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging Random Forest (DM)与Dirichlet-Weighted Random Forest (DW)，通过浓度参数α>0对样本重加权进行调制；并给出一个简单理论判据，说明何时这些变体与标准随机森林无差别，据此设计轻量调参策略。

Result: 在公开分类基准上的受控实验中，DM和DW持续具有竞争力，且往往优于其他随机森林基线，额外运行时间可忽略不计。

Conclusion: DM与DW是有效且高效的随机森林改进方案，理论判据可指导实用调参，在几乎不增加开销的情况下提升性能。

Abstract: We revisit Breiman's observation that reducing inter-tree correlation without weakening individual trees can improve random forests. Building on this principle, we introduce two variants: Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging Random Forest (DM) and Dirichlet-Weighted Random Forest (DW). Both modulate sample reweighting via a concentration parameter $α>0$. We provide a simple theoretical criterion that clarifies when these variants behave indistinguishably from standard random forests, and we use it to guide a lightweight tuning strategy. In a controlled evaluation on public classification benchmarks, DM and DW are consistently competitive and often stronger than other random-forest (RF) baselines, with negligible additional runtime.

</details>


### [151] [How Many Posterior Samples? Calibrated Stopping for Adaptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21813)
*Vincent Corlay,Andriy Enttsel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文聚焦分类导向自适应感知中的"停止层"，将后验样本的类别投票转化为判定，并提出校准的停止规则以控制误判概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验样本的类别标签可作为投票决定是否继续感知，但常用的阈值插入规则在投票质量等于阈值时约有50%概率误判，因此阈值本身并不构成置信保证。

Method: 在不修改后验采样器与感知方向的前提下，校准固定样本规则与有限时域序贯规则至给定误判概率，并研究精确截断（fixed-pool规则在最终判决确定时即停止）；进一步推导单轮判定概率如何决定后验样本成本与分类精度。

Result: 在MNIST上使用DDRM与固定PCA引导探测序列，截断可节省最多62%的后验样本；在匹配工作点下，序贯停止成本最低，且在高精度时可牺牲更多后验样本换取更少测量次数。

Conclusion: 阈值插入规则缺乏置信保证，而校准的序贯停止规则能在控制误判概率的同时显著降低后验采样成本，并可在样本成本与测量次数间灵活权衡。

Abstract: In classification-oriented adaptive sensing, posterior samples characterize uncertainty at the current measurement state and can serve two roles: they may guide the next sensing direction, while their class labels provide votes for the candidate classes and determine whether sensing should continue. We focus on the stopping layer that turns these votes into a declaration, without modifying the posterior sampler or sensing directions. A natural plug-in rule declares when the observed vote share exceeds a threshold. We show that this threshold is not itself a confidence guarantee: when the underlying vote mass equals the threshold, the plug-in rule declares about half the time. As alternatives, we calibrate a fixed-sample rule and a finite-horizon sequential rule to a prescribed false-declaration probability, and study exact curtailment, which stops a fixed-pool rule once its final verdict is forced. We then derive how one-round declaration probabilities determine posterior-sample cost and classification accuracy along a sensing path. On MNIST with DDRM and a fixed PCA-guided probe sequence, curtailment saves up to 62% of posterior samples. Among the evaluated rules at matched operating points, sequential stopping reduces the cost the most. At a high accuracy, that same sequential rule can trade more posterior samples for fewer measurements.

</details>


### [152] [Riemannian Simultaneous Inference for Tangent Vector Field Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21910)
*Xiaotian Chang,Yangdi Jiang,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文研究无边界黎曼流形上的非参切向量场回归，提出核估计器（平行移动+体积校正局部平均），建立一致二阶偏差、有限带宽协方差与随机速率，并构造可行的同时置信管，附模拟与全球风场数据应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同点的响应位于不同切空间，无法直接做局部平均；需要对切向量场进行非参数回归并实现同时（整场）推断。

Method: 核估计器先将邻近响应平行移动到目标切空间，再做体积校正的局部平均；将切范数写成单位切丛上的上确界；通过精确协方差白化得到单位方差高斯场，分析其相关长度（基流形方向~h，纤维方向~1）；结合局部协方差几何导出带显式内蕴常数的Gumbel极限，配合高斯近似与交叉拟合协方差估计构造同时置信管，并讨论带宽选择与高阶偏差校正。

Result: 导出一致二阶偏差、有限带宽协方差与随机速率；证明同时推断的Gumbel极限及显式内蕴常数；得到可行的同时置信管。多种流形上的模拟支持该推断流程，全球风数据随机重构展示置信管截面刻画空间变化的不确定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为流形上切向量场提供了理论上严谨且可实用的同时推断工具，模拟与真实数据均验证其有效性。

Abstract: We consider nonparametric tangent vector field regression on a Riemannian manifold without boundary. Because responses at different points lie in different tangent spaces, the proposed kernel estimator first parallel transports nearby responses to the target tangent space and then forms a volume-corrected local average. We first derive its uniform second-order bias, finite-bandwidth covariance, and stochastic rate. For simultaneous inference, the tangent norm is written as a supremum over the unit tangent bundle. Exact covariance whitening gives a unit-variance Gaussian field whose correlation length is of order $h$ along the base manifold and of order one along the fibre. Its local covariance geometry leads to a Gumbel limit with an explicit intrinsic constant. Combining this limit with Gaussian approximation and cross-fitted covariance estimation yields a feasible simultaneous confidence tube for the regression field. We further discuss improved finite-sample inference with bandwidth selection and high-order bias corrections. Simulations on various manifolds support the proposed inference procedure. A randomized reconstruction of global wind data illustrates how the tube's cross-sections describe spatially varying uncertainty.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Source--Grid Coupling in Power System Oscillations: Observational Equivalence, Feedback Detectability, and Modal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20943)
*Kai Sun,Bin Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文从源-网耦合方向性出发，重新审视强制振荡与自然振荡的区分，建立反馈驱动的动态边界框架，分析强制源与单向自治源的观测等价性及双向反馈的可辨识条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强制/自然振荡区分将振荡源视为固定电力系统边界之外的外部因素，忽视了源-网耦合的方向性，导致对反馈是否存在的判断模糊。作者希望明确何时可从电网侧测量推断源-网反馈。

Method: 提出耦合感知的动态解释，显式考虑源-网耦合方向性；在相同确定性电网初值问题和测量映射下考察规定强迫与单向自治源的精确观测等价性；在增广自治模型中引入双向反馈导致源-网特征值交互；用两模态约化模型分析交互依赖于环路耦合与复模态间隔；并在两区域系统和WECC 243节点系统上仿真验证。

Result: 匹配的界面信号在给定初值问题和测量映射下产生精确等价；双向反馈允许源-网特征值交互，交互强度由环路耦合和复模态间隔共同决定；两区域系统可在标定源族内检测反馈，源模型失配会与反馈混淆；WECC 243节点系统验证了大规模可行性，且反馈检测不要求精确候选源选择。

Conclusion: 源-网耦合方向性构成基于反馈的动态边界，明确了反馈何时可由电网侧测量推断以及弱反馈为何可能无法识别，为振荡源定位与反馈辨识提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The conventional distinction between forced and natural oscillations treats the oscillation source as external to a fixed power-system boundary. This paper develops a coupling-aware dynamical interpretation by explicitly accounting for the directionality of source-grid coupling. It examines when prescribed forcing and a one-way autonomous source are observationally equivalent, and when grid measurements can distinguish the presence of grid-to-source feedback. Matched interface signals yield exact equivalence under the same deterministic grid initial-value problem and measurement map. Bidirectional feedback enables source-grid eigenvalue interaction in the augmented autonomous model, while a reduced two-mode model shows that the interaction depends jointly on loop coupling and complex modal separation. The resulting framework establishes source-grid coupling directionality as a feedback-based dynamical boundary and clarifies when feedback can be inferred from grid-side measurements and why weak feedback may remain unresolved. Simulation studies on a two-area system demonstrate feedback detection within a calibrated source family, while source-model mismatch can be confounded with feedback. A WECC 243-bus system study demonstrates feasibility on a large system and shows that feedback detection need not require exact candidate selection.

</details>


### [154] [Optimal Day-Ahead Scheduling of Fast EV Charging Station With Multi-Stage Battery Degradation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20946)
*Y. Wan,D. Gebbran,R. K. Subroto,T. Dragičević*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出多阶段电池退化建模方法，用于含BESS的EV快充站日前调度，降低运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅用单一电池退化模型描述全生命周期老化，难以准确刻画不同阶段的退化过程。

Method: 提出多阶段电池退化建模方法，并将该模型以计算可行的方式显式集成到自适应优化框架中，用于含BESS的EV快充站日前调度。

Result: 相比单阶段模型，多阶段模型平均降低约2.1%的运营成本；分段数增加时模型误差下降并趋于稳定，成本降低幅度增大并饱和；在其他传统退化模型上同样验证了优势。

Conclusion: 多阶段退化建模能更准确刻画全生命周期退化，并在实际调度中有效降低运营成本，具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: The paper proposes a day-ahead scheduling framework with a novel multi-stage battery degradation modeling method for an electric vehicle (EV) fast charging station (FCS) equipped with a battery energy storage system (BESS). Unlike previous studies, which employ a single battery degradation model to represent the aging process, this paper proposes a novel multi-stage battery degradation modeling method to accurately capture the degradation process across the whole lifespan. Subsequently, the multi-stage model is explicitly integrated into the proposed adaptive optimization framework in a computationally tractable way, thus having important practical implications in the field. The paper provides case studies to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed modeling method on a selected cycle aging model in reducing the operation cost of FCS with BESS operating in different stages. As a result, the overall operation cost with the multi-stage model is around 2.1% on average lower than the single-stage model counterpart. In addition, results show that with the increasing number of divided stages, the model error decreases and becomes stable, while the reduced operation cost compared with the single-stage model increases and saturates. Finally, we apply the multi-stage framework considering other conventional degradation models to show the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [155] [Incremental Stability and Convergence Properties of Discrete-Time Projected Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20955)
*Riccardo Bertollo,S. J. A. M. van den Eijnden,W. P. M. H.,Heemels*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间投影型控制系统的增量稳定性与收敛性分析，提出两类方法并给出小增益条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影型控制（如混合积分-增益系统）的分析主要针对连续时间且为非增量分析，无法反映实际数字实现，需离散时间增量分析以获得更准确的鲁棒性能评估。

Method: 方法一：证明投影控制器在投影度量设计得当时可保持其名义未投影动力学的二次增量稳定性，并据此推导出投影控制器与一般非线性对象互联的增量输入-状态稳定小增益条件；方法二：基于直接Lyapunov方法验证输入仿射分段光滑系统的增量稳定性，可视为经典离散时间Demidovic条件的推广。

Result: 通过多个示例验证结果，并利用非线性Bode图进行性能量化，特别关注一阶投影元件。

Conclusion: 所提出的离散时间增量分析方法能更好反映数字实现并给出更准确的鲁棒性能评估，为投影型控制系统提供理论工具。

Abstract: Projection-based controllers can overcome fundamental limitations of classical linear time-invariant control by modifying the controller's input-output behavior via projection. A key example is given by the hybrid integrator-gain system, a projected integrator, which has recently found successful application in several industrial systems. While prior work on analysis and design of projection-based control systems has primarily focused on the continuous-time setting and non-incremental analysis, a more refined incremental analysis in discrete-time is needed to better reflect actual digital implementation and obtain more accurate (robust) performance assessment. To address this need, this paper considers incremental stability and convergence analysis of discrete-time projection-based control systems. Our first methodology is based on showing that such controllers preserve the quadratic incremental stability of their nominal (unprojected) dynamics, if the projection metric is well-designed. Building on this, we derive a small-gain condition guaranteeing incremental input-to-state stability for interconnections of projected controllers with general nonlinear plants. A second approach is grounded in a direct Lyapunov-based method for verifying incremental stability in input-affine piecewise-smooth systems, which can be seen as an extension of the classical discrete-time Demidovic conditions. We illustrate our results through several examples, and demonstrate performance quantification via nonlinear Bode plots, with a special focus on first-order projection elements.

</details>


### [156] [A Mean-Field Approach for Safe Routing of Multi-Destination Urban Air Mobility Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21093)
*Nameer Fawwaz Ahmed,Cody Fleming,Yasser Shoukry*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文为多目的地UAM网络路由提出最优控制理论框架，用CTMC建模、mean-field流体近似，并证明无队列状态的正不变性，将无限维最优控制转化为有限维代数优化，兼顾时间与多跳效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着UAM系统向高密度运行扩展，自主UAV交通管理需要既易处理又满足安全关键要求的控制框架，而vertiport容量与流量约束下的多目的地路由问题十分复杂。

Method: 将网络建模为目的地条件CTMC以刻画排队、服务与飞行状态间的随机转移；采用mean-field流体近似导出耦合ODE系统动力学；形式化证明无队列状态空间的正不变性；在欠载条件下将无限维连续时间最优控制问题转化为有限维代数优化。

Result: 证明了在特定欠载条件下，初始无队列的系统将永久保持无队列；所得框架能联合优化旅行时间与多跳效率并保证全网稳定性；通过刻画稳态流平衡验证方法，并给出大规模移动系统中安全无拥堵运行的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为受容量与流约束的多目的地UAM网络路由提供了可处理的最优控制理论基础，并为大规模系统的安全无拥堵运行提供了充分条件。

Abstract: As Urban Air Mobility (UAM) systems scale toward high-density operations, managing autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) traffic requires control frameworks that are both tractable and safety-critical. This paper presents a principled optimal control-theoretic foundation for routing in multi-destination UAM networks subject to vertiport capacity and flow constraints. We first model the network as a destination-conditioned Continuous-Time Markov Chain (CTMC) to capture the stochastic transitions between queueing, service, and flight states. To ensure tractability, we employ a mean-field fluid approximation and derive the underlying system dynamics as a set of coupled ordinary differential equations. A key contribution of this work is the formal proof of the positive invariance of the queue-free state space. We demonstrate that under specific underloaded conditions, a system initialized without queues will remain queue-free indefinitely. This result allows us to transform a complex, infinite-dimensional continuous-time optimal control problem into a tractable, finite-dimensional algebraic optimization. The resulting framework jointly optimizes for travel time and multi-hop efficiency while ensuring network-wide stability. We validate the approach by characterizing the steady-state flow equilibria and providing sufficient conditions for safe, congestion-free operation in large-scale mobility systems.

</details>


### [157] [Distribution-Free Budgeted Stealthy Attack Scheduling for Remote State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21148)
*Qazi Mairaj ud din,Sidra Ghayour Bhatti,Qadeer Ahmed*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无分布假设的预算约束隐蔽FDI调度器，用分裂共形校准触发阈值，无需系统矩阵或分布模型，对任意幅值可测检测器实现精确路径隐蔽，并给出有限样本触发率界与稳态退化恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件触发调度器依赖高斯新息尾部反演阈值、用协方差匹配证明隐蔽性，仅在Gaussianity下精确；而真实信息物理系统残差常偏离高斯，导致隐蔽性与预算保证失效。

Method: 将最坏FDI动作与分裂共形校准触发器配对：不依赖被控对象矩阵与任何分布模型；对任意触发规则和新息律建立路径隐蔽性；推导有限样本的触发率无分布界，并在平稳遍历下保证预算几乎必然达成；给出与单一标量能量捕获ψ线性的稳态退化恒等式。

Result: 理论上获得对任意幅值可测检测器的精确路径隐蔽、有限样本触发率界及预算达成性；条件符号对称下证书可扩展到符号敏感检测器，残余暴露由四阶累积量控制（高斯下消失）。蒙特卡洛与重型卡车CAN数据验证了界，并量化了高斯假设在非高斯情形下的代价。

Conclusion: 该分布无关调度器提供了可认证的预算隐蔽FDI调度，揭示了高斯假设的代价，并在真实非高斯残差场景中验证有效。

Abstract: This letter addresses budgeted stealthy false-data-injection (FDI) scheduling against remote state estimation, where a resource-constrained adversary may corrupt at most a fraction $\barΓ$ of transmissions. Existing event-triggered schedulers invert a Gaussian innovation tail to set the firing threshold and certify stealth by covariance matching; both are exact only under Gaussianity, which real cyber-physical residuals routinely violate. We propose a distribution-free scheduler pairing the worst-case FDI action with a split-conformal calibrated trigger, requiring neither the plant matrices nor any distributional model. We establish exact pathwise stealth against every magnitude-measurable detector, for any firing rule and innovation law; a finite-sample distribution-free bound on the mean firing rate, with almost-sure budget attainment under stationarity and ergodicity; and a steady-state degradation identity linear in a single scalar energy capture $ψ$, maximized by the same order statistic that delivers the budget guarantee. A conditional sign-symmetry condition delimits when the certificate extends to sign-sensitive detectors, the residual exposure being governed by a fourth cumulant that vanishes under Gaussian noise. Monte-Carlo studies and a heavy-duty truck CAN record confirm the bounds and quantify what the Gaussian assumption costs outside its regime.

</details>


### [158] [Stochastic MPC under Heavy-Tailed Disturbances: An Extreme Value Theory Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21170)
*Xiuzhen Ye,Wentao Tang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出基于极值理论(EVT)的重尾扰动的随机MPC，并用极值指数θ刻画闭环下的时间聚集，收紧稀有事件概率约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键系统的扰动常呈重尾，极端偏差远超轻尾模型预测；现有SMPC依赖假设分布、矩界或有限场景，在未知重尾下都会退化。

Method: 假设扰动为正则变化(regular varying)，用渐近精确的EVT刻画tube误差尾部；并推导可数据估计的闭式极值指数θ，对预测时域内稀有事件聚集进行θ修正。

Result: θ修正约束能限制整个时域内稀有事件episode的概率，而非仅单步越限概率；在非线性自行车(独轮车)避障、Student-t扰动仿真中验证，安全约束违反频率降低。

Conclusion: 将EVT与极值指数引入SMPC，可更真实地处理重尾扰动及其时间聚集，提升安全约束满足率，且参数可从数据估计。

Abstract: Safety-critical control systems must contend with disturbances whose extreme deviations occur far more frequently than classical light-tailed models predict. Existing stochastic MPC (SMPC) formulations tighten constraints using an assumed distribution, a moment bound, or a finite scenario sample, each of which degrades under an unknown heavy-tailed disturbance. This paper develops an SMPC formulation for linear systems under heavy-tailed disturbances that is only assumed to be regular varying, replacing these approaches with an explicit extreme value theory (EVT) characterization of the tube error tail that is asymptotically exact. We further show that closed-loop dynamics induce temporal clustering of rare excursions across the prediction horizon, and characterize this clustering through a closed-form extremal index estimable from data. The resulting $θ$-corrected constraint bounds the probability of a rare-event episode over the horizon, rather than only the marginal per-step exceedance probability. Simulation on a nonlinear unicycle navigating past an obstacle under Student-$t$ disturbances validates both approaches and demonstrates reduced frequencies of safety constraint violations.

</details>


### [159] [Minimizing Bid Cost Recovery for Energy Storage with Uniform Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21230)
*Yaxuan Yu,Jingguan Liu,Cong Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究滚动窗口调度中储能资源的市场内统一定价与市场外投标成本回收（BCR）支付问题，揭示BCR由跨时段耦合根本驱动，并提出UP-BCR定价方案显著降低BCR与需求侧支付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能因SOC约束导致跨时段耦合，现有LMP可能无法补偿其跨时段机会成本，从而引发市场外BCR支付，需要研究其成因并改进定价机制。

Method: 分析滚动窗口调度下市场内统一定价与市场外BCR；证明供给侧投标高于需求侧投标时正BCR不可避免；识别与SOC约束绑定相关的跨时段耦合指标；在预测不确定性下通过仿真比较BCR最小化统一定价方案（UP-BCR）与现有实时定价方法。

Result: 正BCR仅在跨时段耦合指标活跃时出现，表明BCR由跨时段耦合根本驱动；仿真显示UP-BCR相较LMP大幅降低BCR和需求侧支付，同时在铜板模型下保持零商品剩余。

Conclusion: BCR本质上由跨时段耦合引起，UP-BCR定价方案能在维持零商品剩余的同时有效减少BCR与需求支付。

Abstract: We study in-market uniform pricing and out-of-market bid cost recovery (BCR) payments in rolling-window dispatch for real-time power system operations with energy storage resources (ESRs). Due to intertemporal state-of-charge (SOC) constraints, ESR operations are temporally coupled, and existing in-market locational marginal pricing (LMP) may fail to compensate ESR's intertemporal opportunity costs, thereby triggering out-of-market BCR payments. We show that positive BCR is unavoidable when dispatched generators or ESRs have supply-side bids higher than the demand-side bid. We further identify an intertemporal coupling indicator associated with binding SOC constraints and demonstrate empirically that positive BCR arises only when this indicator is active, revealing that BCR is fundamentally driven by intertemporal coupling. In the simulation, we compare a BCR-minimizing uniform pricing scheme (UP-BCR) with existing real-time pricing methods under forecast uncertainty and show that UP-BCR substantially reduces BCR and demand payments relative to LMP while maintaining zero merchandising surplus in a copper-plate model.

</details>


### [160] [Optimal Regret for Online Storage Control via Cumulative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21262)
*Kamiar Asgari,Michael J. Neely*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究带对抗到达的标量存储系统在线控制，提出精确重参数化与衰减加权投影次梯度更新，实现与记忆长度无关的 $O(\sqrt T)$ 遗憾，并给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量存储系统在对抗性非负到达下，控制器须在到达与成本函数揭示前决策，且需满足资源可用性约束；现有单纯形扰动-动作策略类的遗憾界依赖策略记忆长度，其与时间horizon和保留时间的内在依赖关系尚不明确。

Method: 针对单纯形扰动-动作策略类，用累积分配分数做精确重参数化，并采用衰减加权投影次梯度更新；仅需 $O(\log T)$ 记忆与算术运算、每轮一次成本次梯度查询。另外构造存储专用的分块结构证明下界。

Result: 在保留系数与成本常数固定时，相对最佳固定无限记忆策略达到 $O(\sqrt T)$ 遗憾；针对任意因果可行控制器（含随机控制器）证明匹配下界。令 $\tau=(1-\alpha)^{-1}$，当 $\alpha\in[1/2,1)$、$T\ge4$ 且正成本常数固定时，有限记忆单纯形策略类及其无限记忆扩展的极小极大期望遗憾为 $\Theta(\sqrt T\min\{T,\tau\}^{3/2})$。

Conclusion: 所提重参数化与衰减加权次梯度方法使遗憾与策略记忆长度无关；该工作明确了该类策略基准中时间horizon与保留时间的联合依赖关系。

Abstract: We study online control of a scalar storage system with adversarial nonnegative arrivals, known retention coefficient, and convex costs depending on both state and action. Each action must respect current resource availability and is chosen before the current arrival and cost function are revealed. For the existing simplex disturbance-action policy class, we give an exact reparameterization by cumulative allocation fractions and a decay-weighted projected subgradient update. The resulting regret bound is independent of policy memory length. For fixed retention coefficient and cost constants, the controller achieves $O(\sqrt T)$ regret against the best fixed infinite-memory policy in this class, using $O(\log T)$ memory and arithmetic operations per round and one cost-subgradient query. A storage-specific block construction gives a matching lower bound against every causal feasible controller, including randomized controllers. Writing $τ=(1-α)^{-1}$, the minimax expected regret is $Θ(\sqrt T\min\{T,τ\}^{3/2})$ for every finite-memory simplex policy class and its infinite-memory extension, when $α\in[1/2,1)$, $T\ge4$, and the positive cost constants are fixed. This identifies the joint horizon and retention-time dependence for these policy benchmarks.

</details>


### [161] [Rate-cost Trade-offs in H-infinity Control with Initial State Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21270)
*Vikrant Malik,Victoria Kostina,Babak Hassibi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究数字通信信道下 H-infinity 控制中数据率与性能的根本折衷，针对标量系统给出所需数据率的下界，并证明对数量化是自然选择，提出可达方案且在高数据率极限下渐近匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测器与控制器之间存在数字通信信道，需明确通信数据率与 H-infinity 性能之间的基本权衡关系。

Method: 考虑无加性内部噪声的线性系统（不确定性仅在初始状态）；对标量系统采用确定性的 time-zero covering 论证推导数据率下界，并设计可达编码/控制方案。

Result: 得到给定 H-infinity 代价下所需数据率的下界，说明对数量化（Elia, 2000）是自然选择；可达方案在高数据率极限下速率渐近达到下界。

Conclusion: 标量系统中通信数据率与 H-infinity 性能的折衷可由该下界刻画，且所提方案在高数据率下渐近最优。

Abstract: We consider the problem of H-infinity control in the presence of a digital communication channel between the observer and the controller and investigate the fundamental trade-off between the required communication data rate and H-infinity performance. The system follows linear dynamics and has no additive internal noise, so that the uncertainty is confined to the initial state. For the scalar system, using a deterministic time-zero covering argument, we establish a lower bound on the required data rate for a given H-infinity cost and demonstrate why logarithmic quantization (Elia, 2000) is a natural choice. We further develop an achievability scheme and show that, for the scalar system, its rate matches the converse asymptotically in the high-data-rate limit.

</details>


### [162] [A Distributed Step-by-step Finite-time Consensus Design for Heterogeneous Battery Energy Storage Devices with Droop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21313)
*Yalin Zhang,Yunzhong Song,Shumin Fei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模电力系统中分布式发电机因地理位置分散而难以协同控制的问题，提出了一种用于异构电池储能系统（BESS）的分布式逐步有限时间一致性控制方案，实现有限时间内协调一致，通过分簇、五输入控制和虚拟领导者，达成能量平衡、功率共享及电压频率同步与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电力系统中各发电机分布在不同的地理区域，若不考虑发电机的距离信息，多发电机系统的协同控制难以良好实现，因此需要一种考虑位置信息、能快速协调的分布式控制方案。

Method: 首先根据BESS的位置将其分为若干簇，每簇设有一个负责接收外部信息的主动领导者；随后使用五个功能导向的输入来协调多个BESS，实现能量水平平衡、有功/无功功率共享以及电压/频率同步；进一步引入虚拟领导者（即外部领导者），实现频率和电压恢复到主电网的额定值。该方案完全分布式，每个BESS仅利用自身及邻居的信息。

Result: 该控制方案相比集中式方法完全分布式，且对负载扰动以及通信拓扑的即插即用具有鲁棒性；通过在修改后的IEEE 57节点系统上进行仿真实验，验证了所提方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式逐步有限时间一致性方案能够有效实现异构BESS的协同控制，在有限时间内完成协调一致，并具备分布式、抗扰动和即插即用的优势，适用于电力工程领域。

Abstract: As all generators are distributed in different areas among large scale power systems, the cooperative manipulation of the multi-generator system cannot be done well without consideration of the distance information of the generators, A distributed step-by-step finite-time consensus scheme for the heterogeneous Battery Energy Storage System (BESS) is proposed in this paper, where the coordinated consensus can be come into reality within a limited time, which is appealing for the electrical engineering community. To be concrete, at first, all BESSs are classified into several clusters according to their locations, and in each cluster, there is an active leader in charge of information receiving from outside. Then after, in order to coordinate the multi BESSs, five inputs, which are function oriented, were used to achieve energy level balancing, active/reactive power sharing, and voltage/frequency synchronization of the multi BESSs. To be further, the frequency and voltage restoration to the nominal values of the main grid was made possible by the introduction of a virtual leader, which is actually an external leader. Compared with the centralized methods, this control scheme is entirely distributed, and each BESS only utilizes the information of its own and its neighbors. Besides, this control is robust to the load perturbation and the plug-and-play of the communication topology. Finally, some simulation experiments are executed on the modified IEEE 57-bus system to verify the suggested scheme.

</details>


### [163] [Iterative Single-Loop Coordination of Transmission and Distribution Systems and DER Aggregators with Limited Information Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21435)
*Robert Sosnowski,Yury Dvorkin,Marcin Baszynski,Charalambos Konstantinou*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于近端梯度的单环迭代机制，实现TSO、DSO与DER聚合商之间的去中心化协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DER日益普及，需要在TSO、DSO与聚合商层面进行统一协调。

Method: 透明、可扩展、去中心化的单环迭代机制，信息共享有限；相邻层级基于价格的迭代协调（上级给价格信号，下级回报期望功率）；将近端梯度迭代协同优化算法扩展到三层。

Result: 与集中式方案结果非常接近；相较基于固定输电能价的顺序参考方案显著提升，尤其在DER灵活性较高时。

Conclusion: 所提方法在有限信息共享下实现近集中式效果，具良好可扩展性与收敛性。

Abstract: The growing integration of DERs underscores the importance of unified coordination across TSO, DSO, and aggregator levels. This paper proposes a coordination method between TSO, DSOs and DER aggregators, based on a transparent, well-scalable, and decentralized single-loop iterative mechanism with limited information sharing. The approach enables iterative price-based coordination between adjacent levels, where the higher-level entities provide price signals and the lower-level ones respond with desired power schedules. The proposed method uses the proximal-gradient-based iterative co-optimization algorithm extended to a three-level case, which enjoys convergence characteristic of proximal algorithms. The performance of the proposed approach is compared with both a centralized formulation and a sequential reference formulation based on fixed transmission-level energy prices. The results demonstrate that the proposed coordination achieves outcomes very close to those obtained with the centralized formulation, while significantly improving performance relative to the sequential reference, particularly at higher levels of DER flexibility.

</details>


### [164] [Model-Free Control for Residential Heating: Deployment and Simulation of Nonlinear Data-Enabled Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21500)
*Sebastian Zieglmeier,Chris Verhoek,Jaap Eising,Mathias Hudoba de Badyn*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文将三种非线性数据驱动预测控制（DPC）方法用于住宅供暖，在NEST数字孪生及真实公寓上验证，比传统滞后控制器节能约11%，且非线性方法均优于线性DeePC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅供暖能耗占比大，预测控制可节能，但MPC需为每个单元建模，耗时甚至不可行，且模型难以跨异构建筑迁移。DeePC免建模，但其基于确定性线性时不变系统假设，对住宅供暖不现实。

Method: 采用三种近期提出的非线性DeePC扩展（统称DPC）：Select-DPC、增益调度DPC、线性参数变化DPC；与标准DeePC及行业标准滞后控制器对比，在瑞士NEST研究建筑某有人居住单元的校准数字孪生上运行整个供暖季，并进一步在真实住宅公寓部署增益调度DPC验证。

Result: 各DPC控制器均显著低于滞后控制器能耗（整季约11%），验证了DPC的适用性；非线性方法在所有场景下均优于线性DeePC；论文还分析了各非线性方法的相对成本与收益。

Conclusion: DPC（尤其是非线性扩展）可有效降低住宅供暖能耗，无需精确建模且超越线性DeePC；仿真与真实部署一致。研究还给出三种非线性方法的成本-收益对比。

Abstract: Residential heating accounts for a large share of building energy use, and predictive control strategies can reduce it by anticipating rather than reacting to the room temperature alone. The usual manner of implementing predictive control, model predictive control (MPC), requires an accurate model of each individual residential unit for heating control. Obtaining this model is time-consuming to obtain manually or even impossible, and such models do not transfer across a heterogeneous building stock. Data-enabled predictive control (DeePC) removes this modeling step by designing the controller directly using measured trajectories of the system. The foundations of DeePC, however, are built on the class of deterministic linear time-invariant systems, which is an unrealistic assumption for residential heating. This work applies three recently proposed nonlinear extensions of DeePC, collectively referred to as data-driven predictive control (DPC): Select-DPC, gain-scheduling DPC, and linear parameter-varying DPC. These are compared against standard DeePC and the hysteresis controller, the industry standard in residential heating. The comparison runs over a full heating season on a calibrated digital twin of an occupied research unit, the NEST research building in Switzerland, assessed by heating energy and comfort-band violation. Moreover, gain-scheduling DPC was deployed on the real residential apartment and validated the conclusions from the simulations. Each of the DPC controllers consumes significantly less energy than the hysteresis controller, amounting to roughly $11\%$ over the season, validating the use of DPC. The nonlinear methods further outperform linear DeePC in all assessed scenarios, and we analyze the relative costs and benefits of each nonlinear method.

</details>


### [165] [Response Time Stochastic Analysis for Fixed-Priority Stable Real-Time Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21760)
*Kevin Zagalo,Yasmina Abdeddaïm,Avner Bar-Hen,Liliana Cucu-Grosjean*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文证明平均系统利用率小于1是实时系统可行性的必要条件，定义此类系统为稳定系统，并证明Liu与Layland定理在具有隐式截止期的稳定概率实时系统中成立，给出响应时间的解析近似及系统进入稳态时刻的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时系统可行性分析多基于确定性假设，但实际系统存在概率性行为。作者希望将经典实时理论（如Liu和Layland定理）推广到概率实时系统，并刻画其从瞬态到稳态的演化过程。

Method: 通过理论证明：首先证明平均系统利用率小于1是实时系统可行性的必要条件（稳定系统）；指出稳定系统存在瞬态与稳态两个状态；证明Liu与Layland定理对隐式截止期的稳定概率实时系统成立；并推导两种状态下响应时间的解析近似以及系统进入稳态时刻的界。

Result: 证明了平均利用率小于1是可行性的必要条件；证明Liu与Layland定理在稳定概率实时系统中成立；给出了瞬态与稳态响应时间的解析近似；给出了系统达到稳态时刻的界。

Conclusion: 该工作为概率实时系统的可行性分析提供了理论基础，扩展了经典实时调度理论，并揭示了稳定系统瞬态与稳态的响应时间特性。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that a mean system utilization smaller than one is a necessary condition for the feasibility of real-time systems. Such systems are defined as stable. Stable systems have two distinct states: a transient state, followed by a steady-state where the same distribution of response times is repeated infinitely for each task. We prove that the Liu and Layland theorem holds for stable probabilistic real-time systems with implicit deadlines, we provide an analytical approximation of response times for each of those two states and a bound of the instant when a real-time system becomes steady.

</details>


### [166] [Adaptive Uncertainty-Aware Modeling and Stochastic Radial Basis Function Predictive Control for Personalized Fluid Resuscitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21821)
*Elham Estiri,Hossein Mirinejad*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯生理建模与最优控制结合的框架，实现不确定性感知、个性化的液体复苏血压调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体复苏中血压调控存在测量噪声（偶然不确定性）和患者生理差异（认知不确定性），需要不确定性感知的个性化建模与控制。

Method: 先构建UVAE-SSM捕捉MAP与输液动态并建模偶然不确定性；再用BNN构建BNSSM建模认知不确定性，生成虚拟患者；设计sRBF-MPC在生理约束下追踪MAP目标；并用流式VPG数据在线微调实现渐进个性化。

Result: 在未见动物和独立人类数据集上，UVAE-SSM与BNSSM预测精度高、跨群体泛化强；闭环实验中sRBF-MPC实现稳定MAP调控，风险感知优于Q-MPC和sQ-MPC。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在线模型适应处理患者间与患者内差异，为重症监护中不确定性感知、个性化血流动力学建模与控制迈出有希望的一步。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework integrating Bayesian physiological modeling with optimal control strategies to achieve uncertainty-aware, personalized hemodynamic regulation during fluid resuscitation. An uncertainty-aware variational autoencoder state-space model (UVAE-SSM) was first developed to capture the dynamical relationship between mean arterial pressure (MAP) and fluid infusion using limited data, while explicitly modeling aleatoric uncertainty (i.e., randomness in the measurements, such as sensor noise). Then, a Bayesian nonlinear state-space model (BNSSM) was developed by utilizing Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) to capture epistemic uncertainty arising from physiological and patient-specific variability, enabling the creation of a virtual patient generator (VPG). Building on this uncertainty-aware modeling framework, a stochastic radial basis function model predictive control (sRBF-MPC) algorithm was designed to track the MAP target while satisfying physiological constraints. Finally, an online fine-tuning algorithm was developed to adapt the nominal UVAE-SSM using streaming VPG data, enabling progressive personalization during closed-loop therapy. Simulation results across unseen animal subjects and an independent human clinical dataset demonstrated the strong predictive accuracy and cross-population generalizability of the UVAE-SSM and BNSSM models. Closed-loop evaluations confirmed that the proposed sRBF-MPC framework achieved stable MAP regulation while providing better risk-aware control compared to quadratic MPC (Q-MPC) and stochastic quadratic MPC (sQ-MPC). Overall, the proposed framework accounts for inter- and intra-patient variability through online model adaptation, offering a promising step toward uncertainty-aware, personalized hemodynamic modeling and control in critical care.

</details>


### [167] [Distributed Multiconsensus Control of BESSs Based on Centrality of Eigenvectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21856)
*Yalin Zhang,Zhongxin Liu,Zengqiang Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对下垂控制电池储能系统(BESS)，基于特征向量中心性设计通信权重分配方法，实现分布式二次电压控制与SoC均衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次控制和SoC均衡是BESS的重要控制目标；传统方法可能需要多个领导者、且需在通信网络中传输容量/下垂系数信息，增加通信负担。

Method: 针对连通有向图，基于特征向量中心性设计通信权重分配，使邻接矩阵具有给定特征向量；随后设计分布式二次电压控制器与SoC均衡控制器，并分析控制增益以保证稳定性。

Result: 电压二次控制仅需单个领导者即可实现电压多一致性；容量/下垂系数无需在通信网络中传输即可按容量实现功率分配与SoC均衡；仿真验证方案有效性。

Conclusion: 所提通信权重分配与分布式控制方案在减少通信需求的同时实现了电压多一致性与SoC均衡，仿真结果验证其有效性。

Abstract: Secondary control and the State-of-Charge (SoC) balance control are important control objectives for battery energy storage systems (BESSs). In this brief, a communication weight allocation method based on the centrality of eigenvectors is designed for a connected and directed graph, which results in the adjacency matrix having a given eigenvector. Subsequently, a distributed secondary voltage controller and an SoC balancing controller are designed for droop-controlled BESSs to achieve voltage leader-following multiconsensus and SoC balancing, respectively. It is worth mentioning that under the designed voltage secondary control scheme, only a single leader is needed to achieve voltage multiconsensus control. In addition, the capacity information/droop coefficient does not need to be transmitted in the communication network to achieve power sharing according to capacity and SoC balance. For SoC balance control, the control gain is also well analyzed to ensure stability. The relevant simulations verify the effectiveness of the designed scheme.

</details>


### [168] [Improved Droop Control in DC Microgrids via Voltage-Locked Loop Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21913)
*Ognjen Stanojev,Jovan Krajacic,Enea Bianda,Orcun Karaca,Mario Schweizer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于电压锁相环同步的改进下垂控制方法，通过将控制分为虚拟直流电机（VDCM）与并联虚拟电流源，实现直流微电网电压稳定、瞬态响应显著改善及负载均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流微电网需在预设限值内维持直流电压并保证稳定性；传统下垂控制虽可实现分散电压控制与功率分担，但瞬态性能差，在AI数据中心等快速变化负载下会产生电压跌落和超调。

Method: 提出基于电压锁相环同步的改进下垂控制。核心设计将控制方案功能分离为两部分：作为"飞轮"的虚拟直流电机（VDCM），提供瞬态响应以阻尼并稳定直流母线电压变化；并联的虚拟电流源提供缓慢的下垂响应，确保稳态功率平衡。

Result: 在实验微电网装置上验证了所提方法的性能，实现了直流电压稳定、瞬态响应显著改善，并在可用电源变换器之间实现期望的负载分担。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过VDCM与虚拟电流源的并行功能分离，兼顾瞬态阻尼与稳态功率平衡，有效克服传统下垂控制瞬态性能差的缺陷，适用于快速变化负载场景。

Abstract: DC microgrids are low- or medium-voltage networks designed to connect and manage DC-based sources and loads. A key challenge in operating DC microgrids is maintaining the DC voltage within certain predefined limits while ensuring its stability. Droop control, the most common method towards addressing this challenge, enables decentralized voltage control and power sharing, but suffers from poor transient performance, resulting in voltage dips and overshoots in applications with fast varying loads such as AI datacenters. This paper introduces an improved droop control method based on voltage-locked loop synchronization, which ensures DC voltage stabilization with significantly improved transient response and achieves the desired load sharing between the available source converters. The core design principle is reflected in the functional separation of the control scheme into a virtual DC machine (VDCM) that operates as a spinning wheel and a virtual current source connected in parallel. The transient response is provided by the VDCM to damp and stabilize DC bus voltage variations, while the slow droop response is provided by the virtual current source to ensure steady-state power balance. The performance of the proposed method is validated in an experimental microgrid setup.

</details>


### [169] [Decentralized Continuification Control of Multi-Agent Systems via Distributed Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14119)
*Beniamino Di Lorenzo,Gian Carlo Maffettone,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于continuification策略的去中心化控制方法，用核密度估计结合PI一致性动态实现分布式密度估计，使智能体仅依赖邻居信息即可对大规模多智能体系统进行密度控制，效果接近集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统continuification方法虽将ODEs/SDEs智能体模型转为更易处理的PDEs来控制大规模多智能体系统，但需要集中式的宏观状态观测信息，限制了实际应用。作者希望消除这一集中式依赖，实现去中心化控制。

Method: 针对单位圆上的大规模多智能体系统，开发分布式密度估计框架：将核密度估计与PI一致性动态相结合，使各智能体基于通信网络中的邻居信息计算局部密度估计，并据此推导局部控制动作，形成continuification策略的去中心化实现。

Result: 在调节、跟踪以及时变通信拓扑等多个场景下的数值验证表明，该方法有效，且去中心化实现的性能可与集中式方法相媲美，同时提升了可靠性与实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该去中心化continuification实现无需集中式宏观信息即可有效控制大规模多智能体系统密度，性能接近集中式方案，并更具可靠性和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel decentralized implementation of a continuification-based strategy to control the density of large-scale multi-agent systems on the unit circle. While continuification methods effectively address micro-to-macro control problems by reformulating ordinary/stochastic differential equations (ODEs/SDEs) agent-based models into more tractable partial differential equations (PDEs), they traditionally require centralized knowledge of macroscopic state observables. We overcome this limitation by developing a distributed density estimation framework that combines kernel density estimation with PI consensus dynamics. Our approach enables agents to compute local density estimates and derive local control actions using only information from neighboring agents in a communication network. Numerical validations across multiple scenarios - including regulation, tracking, and time-varying communication topologies - confirm the effectiveness of the proposed approach. They also convincingly demonstrate that our decentralized implementation achieves performance comparable to centralized approaches while enhancing reliability and practical applicability.

</details>


### [170] [Interconnections of Dissipative Networks Through a Dynamic Scattering Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21934)
*Ilia G. Polushin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究耗散网络的模块化散射型设计问题，提出一种动态散射型互联控制器设计方法，保证互联系统内部稳定并保持子系统的基本性质，从而支持大规模耗散网络的模块化迭代构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模耗散网络的构建需要模块化方法，使得整体系统在互联后仍保持稳定性和原有性质，同时希望提供灵活的控制器设计与信号选择空间。

Method: 在子系统的基本假设下，设计一种动态的、基于散射（scattering-based）的互联控制器，保证互联结果内部稳定，并继承各子系统的同一组基本性质。

Result: 所提方法保证互联系统内部稳定且保持子系统基本性质，支持该技术的迭代应用，从而可模块化构建大规模耗散网络，并在控制器设计和互联用输入/输出信号选择上具有较大灵活性。

Conclusion: 该散射型模块化设计方法为大规模耗散网络的构建提供了可行且灵活的途径，通过迭代互联实现系统扩展。

Abstract: The problem of modular scattering-based design of dissipative networks is addressed. Under basic assumptions imposed on subsystems, design of a dynamic scattering-based interconnection controller is proposed which guarantees that the resulting interconnection is internally stable and possesses the same set of basic properties as the individual subsystems. This enables iterative application of the developed technique, which allows for building of large-scale dissipative networks in a modular fashion. The proposed method provides substantial flexibility in the controller design as well as in the choice of input and output signals used for interconnections.

</details>


### [171] [Semi-discrete Optimal Transport for Time-Varying Multi-Agent Coverage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21753)
*Italo Napolitano,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出半离散最优传输框架用于时变覆盖控制，通过跟踪最小化瞬时Wasserstein距离的一阶最优性条件实现，最优性残差指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统覆盖控制算法针对静态目标密度，但环境监测、监视和自适应传感等应用涉及时变密度。虽然Voronoi框架下研究过时变覆盖，但将近期静态覆盖控制的最优传输表述推广到时变目标密度仍是未解决问题。

Method: 提出半离散最优传输框架，智能体跟踪与最小化到演化目标密度的瞬时Wasserstein距离相关的一阶最优性条件。方法基于控制智能体位置和定义Laguerre区域的对偶变量的耦合微分方程组，并推导了去中心化近似。

Result: 最优性残差指数收敛到零，在一维域上建立了全局收敛性。数值仿真表明相比准静态和基于Voronoi的方法，跟踪性能有所改善。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将最优传输覆盖控制推广到时变密度场景，具有良好的收敛性和跟踪性能。

Abstract: Coverage control algorithms have traditionally focused on static target densities, where agents optimally cover a fixed spatial distribution. However, many applications, such as environmental monitoring, surveillance, and adaptive sensing, involve time-varying densities. While time-varying coverage has been studied in Voronoi-based frameworks, extending recent optimal transport formulations of static coverage control to time-varying target densities remains an open problem. This paper presents a semi-discrete optimal transport framework for time-varying coverage control, in which agents track the first-order optimality conditions associated with minimizing the instantaneous Wasserstein distance from an evolving target density. The proposed approach is based on a coupled system of differential equations governing agent positions and the dual variables defining Laguerre regions. The resulting optimality residuals converge exponentially to zero, with global convergence established for one-dimensional domains. We also derive decentralized approximations and numerical simulations demonstrate improved tracking performance over quasi-static and Voronoi-based methods.

</details>


### [172] [Multi-Agent Event-Triggered LQG Control under Shared Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21974)
*Zahra Hashemi,Dipankar Maity*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究受限通信网络下多智能体系统的LQG事件触发控制，提出集中式滚动时域调度、MILP建模、跳步剪枝与拍卖式调度器，在性能与通信/计算成本间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体通过容量受限的共享网络通信，虽然动力学解耦，但传输决策因网络约束而耦合，形成受限多智能体调度难题。

Method: 证明最优控制律保持确定性等价并可通过独立有限时域Riccati递推解耦；据此构建集中式滚动时域调度框架，利用估计误差协方差闭式刻画将其转化为MILP；提出窗口跳步剪枝条件提前固定连续零传输决策，并设计基于单步传输收益的拍卖启发式调度器。

Result: MPC调度器在控制性能与通信成本间实现最佳权衡；拍卖调度器以更低计算复杂度逼近MPC性能。

Conclusion: 控制解耦与调度耦合的结构分离为高效调度算法提供了基础，兼顾可扩展性与性能。

Abstract: This letter studies event-triggered linear-quadratic-Gaussian (LQG) control for multi-agent systems sharing a communication network with limited per-step capacity. Although the agent dynamics are decoupled, the communication decisions are coupled through the shared network constraint, leading to a constrained multi-agent scheduling problem. We show that the optimal control law remains certainty-equivalent and decouples across agents through independent finite-horizon Riccati recursions, whereas the transmission schedule remains globally coupled. Based on this structure, we develop a centralized receding-horizon scheduling framework and reformulate the resulting problem as a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) using a closed-form characterization of the estimation-error covariance. To improve scalability, we derive a window-based skip-pruning condition that safely fixes consecutive transmission decisions to zero before solving the MILP, and we propose an auction-inspired scheduler based on one-step transmission-benefit scores. Numerical results show that the proposed model predictive control (MPC) scheduler achieves the best trade-off between control performance and communication cost, while the auction-based scheduler attains performance close to MPC with substantially lower computational complexity.

</details>


### [173] [Robust Macroscopic Density Control of Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09695)
*Gian Carlo Maffettone,Davide Salzano,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的宏观密度控制框架，直接在对流-扩散偏微分方程层面设计反馈律，实现大规模智能体集群在未知扰动下的全局指数收敛控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群协调、交通流等现代应用需协调大规模智能体，而其运行环境非结构化，扰动与未建模动态不可避免，现有方法难以在大规模场景下鲁棒地实现期望的集体行为。

Method: 在密度空间建立控制问题，直接针对对流-扩散偏微分方程设计反馈控制律，显式考虑异构智能体动力学、未建模行为与环境扰动，并针对有界未知漂移项做渐近抑制。

Result: 理论上证明了在L2空间中对期望行为的全局指数收敛，并保证对未知有界漂移的渐近抑制；数值实验覆盖异构振子、交通系统及部分未知环境下的集群机器人，验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模智能体集群在非结构化、受扰动环境中的宏观密度控制提供了可扩展且具理论保证的解决方案，兼具鲁棒性与通用性。

Abstract: Modern applications, such as orchestrating the collective behavior of robotic swarms or traffic flows, require the coordination of large groups of agents evolving in unstructured environments, where disturbances and unmodeled dynamics are unavoidable. In this work, we develop a scalable macroscopic density control framework in which a feedback law is designed directly at the level of an advection--diffusion partial differential equation. We formulate the control problem in the density space and prove global exponential convergence towards the desired behavior in $\mathcal{L}^2$ with guaranteed asymptotic rejection of bounded unknown drift terms, explicitly accounting for heterogeneous agent dynamics, unmodeled behaviors, and environmental perturbations. Our theoretical findings are corroborated by numerical experiments spanning heterogeneous oscillators, traffic systems, and swarm robotics in partially unknown environments.

</details>


### [174] [Learning-Based Augmentation and Adaptation for Grid Sim-to-Real Model Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21986)
*Sayak Mukherjee,Kyung-Bin Kwon,Ramij R. Hossain,Marcelo Elizondo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出学习增强的混合方法，用AI残差模型补充物理仿真模型，捕捉电网真实动态差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型逆变器资源、大负荷、未建模动态和参数漂移等不确定性，使运营商仿真模型与实际电网动态出现偏差，导致控制室关键振荡无法在暂态研究中被捕捉。

Method: 在物理仿真模型基础上，利用PMU/PoW感知轨迹数据训练AI残差模型（backbone编码器+多头解码器处理异构通道），并构建持续学习驱动的自适应框架以在电网模型变化时更新残差模型。

Result: 在IEEE 68节点基准模型上针对多种扰动进行大量仿真，探索了循环学习器、隐式神经常微分方程和Transformer等先进预测架构，验证了残差学习与自适应能力。

Conclusion: 物理模型提供可解释性与结构一致性，学习残差捕捉非理想性偏差，二者结合可有效提升电网动态建模与预测能力。

Abstract: Modern power systems can encounter increased discrepancy between the operators' simulation model and the actual true dynamics of the grid, driven by uncertainties caused by integration of new inverter-based resources (IBRs), large loads, unmodeled dynamics, parameter drifts, etc., to name a few. All of these impact the control room operations, where some critical oscillations may not be captured during the transient studies. To circumvent these issues, we propose a learning-augmented hybrid approach where the operator simulation model is supplemented with artificial intelligence (AI)-learned residual models using the phasor measurement unit (PMU)/ point-on-wave (PoW) based sensed trajectory data. The physics-based operator model provides interpretability and structural consistency, while the learned residual captures discrepancies caused by non-idealities. The learned model employs advanced neural architectures and consists of a backbone encoder and multi-head decoder layers for heterogeneous grid channels. Subsequently, we formulated a continual learning-motivated adaptation framework such that the baseline residual AI model can also be updated when the underlying real grid model changes in future conditions. Extensive numerical simulations are performed on the IEEE 68-bus benchmark model with a diverse set of disturbances, and different state-of-the-art predictive architectures involving recurrent learners, latent neural ODEs, and transformers are explored to demonstrate both residual learning and adaptation capabilities.

</details>


### [175] [Robust multi-scale leader-follower control of large multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16680)
*Davide Salzano,Gian Carlo Maffettone,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文为受扰动的领导者-跟随者多智能体系统设计了宏观密度反馈控制律，实现跟随者密度全局渐近收敛到目标分布，并给出可行性所需的领导者-跟随者质量比下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际多智能体系统（如交通网络、人群疏散）无法对所有智能体施加控制，只能通过可控领导者借助局部交互间接引导不可控跟随者。现有研究仅分别处理简单无扰动系统的领导-跟随密度控制，或单一直接驱动群体的鲁棒密度控制，尚未将二者结合。

Method: 推导受未知有界扰动下领导者与跟随者的耦合连续介质描述，设计宏观反馈律；利用奇异摄动理论分析领导-跟随系统的耦合稳定性，并推导保证可行性的领导者与跟随者质量比的显式下界。

Result: 理论上证明了跟随者密度对目标分布的全局渐近收敛，并得到质量比可行性下界；在异质有偏随机游走者上的数值仿真验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了领导者-跟随密度控制与鲁棒密度控制之间的空白，为仅能通过部分领导者间接控制受扰动多智能体群体提供了理论框架与可行性条件。

Abstract: In many multi-agent systems of practical interest, such as traffic networks or crowd evacuation, control actions cannot be exerted on all agents. Instead, controllable leaders must indirectly steer uncontrolled followers through local interactions. Existing results address either leader-follower density control of simple, unperturbed multi-agent systems or robust density control of a single directly actuated population, but not their combination. We bridge this gap by deriving a coupled continuum description for leaders and followers subject to unknown bounded perturbations, and designing a macroscopic feedback law that guarantees global asymptotic convergence of the followers' density to a desired distribution. The coupled stability of the leader-follower system is analyzed via singular perturbation theory, and an explicit lower bound on the leader-to-follower mass ratio required for feasibility is derived. Numerical simulations on heterogeneous biased random walkers validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [176] [Internal-Model-Control-Based Virtual Admittance Emulation for Enhanced Grid-Forming Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22037)
*Ruzica Cvetanovic,Lazar Stojanovic,Paolo Sbabo,Paolo Mattavelli,Massimo Bongiorno*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内模控制(IMC)的虚拟导纳(VA)内环控制方法，用于构网型变流器(GFM)，使端口导纳在外部环影响范围外精确匹配目标导纳，避免因电压前馈低通滤波器高截止频率引起的小信号谐波不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源大规模接入，构网型变流器成为解决稳定性与控制难题的关键。基于虚拟导纳的内环控制可灵活塑造变流器端口小信号导纳，但现有(state-of-the-art)VA控制未充分考虑内环电流控制与电压前馈的影响，可能在谐波频段引发小信号不稳定。

Method: 提出一种基于内模控制(IMC)的虚拟导纳方法，内在计及内环电流控制回路与电压前馈的影响；在外部环影响范围外的频率上，使变流器端口导纳贴合目标导纳，并分析传统VA控制中电压前馈低通滤波器截止频率对稳定性的作用。

Result: 揭示传统VA控制在采用高截止频率低通滤波器进行电压前馈时可能引发谐波频段的小信号失稳；而所提VA控制无论该截止频率如何都能保证稳定运行。实验在三相电压源变流器实验室样机上验证。

Conclusion: 所提IMC-based VA控制能精确塑造端口导纳、提升稳定性，并对电压前馈滤波截止频率具有鲁棒性，已通过实验验证。

Abstract: The large-scale integration of renewable energy into electrical power systems places a growing reliance on grid-forming converters (GFMs) to overcome critical stability and control hurdles. By emulating a slowly varying voltage source behind a tunable resistive?inductive impedance, GFMs enable precise setpoint tracking and vital grid support. In this regard, virtual admittance (VA)-based inner-loop control provides exceptional flexibility in shaping the converter's small-signal immittance at connection terminals, allowing for advanced GFM functionalities. This article addresses a novel internal-model-control-based VA approach, which inherently accounts for the effects of the inner current-control loop and the voltage feedforward. Unlike the state-of-the-art VA control, the proposed VA control ensures that, at frequencies outside the influence of outer loops, the converter's terminal admittance closely matches the target admittance, which is shown to be relevant for stability. It is revealed how, with the state-of-the art VA control, a small-signal instability in the harmonic range may arise if a low-pass filter with a high cut-off frequency is used for the voltage feedforward. However, the proposed VA control ensures stable operation regardless of this cut-off frequency. The methodology is experimentally validated using a laboratory prototype that features three-phase voltage-source converters.

</details>


### [177] [Leader-Follower Density Control of Multi-Agent Systems with Interacting Followers: Feasibility and Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11353)
*Beniamino Di Lorenzo,Gian Carlo Maffettone,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究leader-follower大规模多智能体系统的密度控制问题，考虑follower间的相互作用，基于PDE宏观框架给出可行性条件与反馈控制律，并揭示相变阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作忽视follower之间的相互作用，而现实系统（如群体聚集、避碰）中这种交互至关重要，需在密度控制中加以刻画。

Method: 在leader-follower设定下，以PDE描述follower密度动态的宏观框架，显式引入follower-follower交互，推导可行性条件并设计反馈控制律。

Result: (i)给出联系目标分布与交互强度、扩散、leader质量的充要可行性条件；(ii)反馈控制律保证局部稳定并给出吸引域显式估计；揭示尖锐可行性阈值与无法控制的相变。

Conclusion: 一维、二维宏观仿真验证理论，基于有限种群的智能体仿真证实框架的实际可部署性。

Abstract: We address density control problems for large-scale multi-agent systems in leader-follower settings, where a group of controllable leaders must steer a population of followers toward a desired spatial distribution. Unlike prior work, we explicitly account for follower-follower interactions, capturing realistic behaviors such as flocking and collision avoidance. Within a macroscopic framework based on partial differential equations governing the density dynamics, we derive (i) necessary and sufficient feasibility conditions linking the target distribution to interaction strength, diffusion, and leader mass, and (ii) a feedback control law guaranteeing local stability with an explicit estimate of the basin of attraction. Our analysis reveals sharp feasibility thresholds, phase transitions beyond which no control effort can achieve the desired configuration. Numerical simulations in one- and two-dimensional domains validate the theoretical results at the macroscopic level, and agent-based simulations on finite populations confirm the practical deployability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [178] [Entropy-Regularized Optimal Transport for Time-Varying Multi-Agent Coverage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09829)
*Italo Napolitano,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵正则化半离散最优传输的多智能体时变覆盖控制方法，通过跟踪演化的一阶最优性条件实现指数收敛的反馈-前馈控制，并分析正则化参数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无正则化方法采用硬Laguerre划分（Voronoi），控制律设计和数值实现较复杂，需处理不连续的划分边界。本文旨在用熵正则化简化控制律设计与实现。

Method: 将时变覆盖控制建模为跟踪演化目标密度的熵正则化半离散最优传输问题。熵正则化使每个点的质量按比例分配给所有智能体（而非硬划分），据此推导跟踪演化一阶最优性条件的反馈-前馈控制器，实现指数收敛，并研究正则化参数的作用。

Result: 正则化参数超过临界阈值时，完全塌缩的配置成为局部最优。数值实验表明：中等正则化下性能与无正则化方法相当，并优于基于Voronoi的覆盖基线。

Conclusion: 熵正则化在保持性能的同时简化了控制律设计与数值实现，但需将正则化参数控制在临界阈值以下以避免塌缩解。

Abstract: This paper addresses time-varying coverage control for multi-agent systems, formulated as the tracking of an evolving target density via entropy-regularized semi-discrete optimal transport. Unlike the hard Laguerre partition of the unregularized formulation, entropic regularization assigns fractions of the mass at each point to all agents, simplifying the design and numerical implementation of the control law. We derive a feedback-feedforward controller that tracks the evolving first-order optimality conditions with exponential convergence, and we investigate the role of the regularization parameter, which, above a critical threshold, renders the fully collapsed configuration locally optimal. Numerical experiments validate the theory: for moderate regularization, the proposed approach has performance comparable to the unregularized formulation and outperforms the corresponding Voronoi-based coverage baseline.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.MF'></div>

# q-fin.MF [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Nested Clustered Optimization Is One End of a Schur Bridge, and the Interior Is Sometimes Provably Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21271)
*Peter Cotton*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文研究聚类投资组合优化：嵌套聚类优化、块求逆（Schur补）与Vecchia近似之间的关系，并提出阻尼Schur补的桥接方法，避免大规模线性求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在每个簇内和簇间分别优化时，如何高效求解全局最小方差组合？块求逆给出两级结构，但完整条件化涉及所有其他资产，计算昂贵。作者希望用更稀疏的条件集（如Vecchia近似）简化跨簇依赖，并建立与全局最优之间的连续桥接。

Method: 提出嵌套聚类优化；用Schur补替换每块以刻画无约束最小方差组合的两级形式；仅条件化其他簇各一个“结点”得到Vecchia式截断，并给出使其精确的秩一跨簇依赖模型；用γ∈[0,1]阻尼Schur补，γ=0对应嵌套聚类优化，γ=1对应全局最优，线性求解规模不超过单簇或簇数。

Result: 在估计误差下，最优γ可严格位于内部，完全耦合（γ=1）仍可能最优；给出最小方差端的局部定理及两种情况的精确例子，包括一个最优点有闭式解的对称族。

Conclusion: 阻尼Schur补提供了从聚类近似到全局最优的可调桥接，计算可控，且在估计误差下最优耦合强度需权衡，不一定取全局耦合。

Abstract: Nested clustered optimization allocates within each cluster from the cluster's own covariance block and then across the resulting cluster portfolios. Block inversion says the unconstrained minimum-variance portfolio has the same two-tier shape, with each block replaced by its Schur complement against every other asset. Conditioning instead on one knot from each other cluster truncates the conditioning set in the manner of a Vecchia approximation, and we give the rank-one model of cross-cluster dependence under which it is exact. Damping the complement by $γ\in[0,1]$ then gives a bridge with nested clustered optimization at $γ=0$ and the global optimum at $γ=1$, with no linear solve larger than a cluster or the number of clusters. Under estimation error the optimal $γ$ can be strictly interior and full coupling can remain optimal, and we give the local theorem at the minimum-variance end with exact examples of both, including a symmetric family in which the optimum is a closed form.

</details>


### [180] [Stochastic Mortality Model with Fractional Lévy Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21232)
*Congxin He,Lilian Hu,Yue Kuen Kwok,Yifan Ye*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将分数Lévy过程与标准布朗运动混合的随机死亡率模型，兼顾长记忆性、解析可处理性与Lévy设定的灵活性，并通过SVD近似及残差修正实现长寿衍生品的高效数值定价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量实证证据显示，忽略长程依赖的随机死亡率模型会低估预期寿命，进而对养老金计划与融资安排产生重大影响，因此需要一个能刻画长记忆特征的死亡率模型。

Method: 构建分数Lévy过程与标准布朗运动相混合的随机死亡率模型，具备精算估值上的解析可处理性和底层Lévy设定的灵活性；为应对分数Lévy过程的非马尔可夫性带来的计算难题，构造有效的奇异值分解(SVD)近似方案进行长寿衍生品定价，并用残差修正方案降低截断误差。

Result: 模型内嵌的长程依赖特征在二战和COVID-19死亡率冲击的实证研究中得到良好体现；SVD近似配合残差修正可有效降低截断误差，实现高效数值定价。

Conclusion: 该混合分数Lévy死亡率模型能有效刻画长记忆性并保持计算可行性，为长寿风险的精算估值与衍生品定价提供了实用工具。

Abstract: A substantial body of empirical evidence suggests that stochastic mortality models ignoring long range dependence tend to underestimate life expectancy, which may lead to profound implications for pension schemes and funding arrangements. This paper addresses the modelling of stochastic mortality via a mixture of a fractional Lévy process and standard Brownian motion. Our stochastic mortality model exhibits nice analytical tractability in actuarial valuations and flexibility in the choice of underlying Lévy specifications. The long range dependence feature embedded in our stochastic mortality model is well reflected in our empirical studies on the mortality shocks during World War II and COVID-19. For efficient numerical pricing of longevity derivatives, we construct an effective singular value decomposition approximation scheme to overcome the computational challenges arising from the non-Markovian nature of the fractional Lévy process. Truncation errors in singular value decomposition approximation can be reduced by an effective residual correction scheme.

</details>


### [181] [Equilibrium prices under hidden Markov fundamentals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21684)
*Henri Pagès,Dylan Possamaï,Mateo Rodriguez Polo*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 研究带有几何红利与隐藏有限状态马尔可夫漂移的Epstein-Zin代表性代理人经济，证明均衡估值的正因子为常数（信念-马尔可夫价格），并在两状态下给出定价方程解的存在唯一性、光滑性与期权定价结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在投资者无法直接观测状态、只能通过信念推断隐藏马尔可夫漂移的经济中，带有Epstein-Zin偏好与几何红利的一般均衡价格结构，尤其允许估值因子偏离常数时均衡会如何约束其形式。

Method: 构建代表性代理人Epstein-Zin经济，设价格-红利比含一个正的绝对连续估值因子；在给定类C与正则性、可容许性、正性条件下证明该因子必为常数；在两状态情形下分析定价方程正解的解析性质；对0<θ≤1证明均衡存在性；在η>0子区域推导信念依赖的股票波动率、欧式期权PDE与短期风险中性对数收益偏度展开。

Result: 证明均衡迫使额外估值因子为常数，得到信念-马尔可夫价格；两状态下给出定价方程正解的存在性、唯一性、端点光滑性、内部解析性与一致界；0<θ≤1时在满足单边组合条件的连续短期利率下支持均衡；并给出信念依赖波动率、期权定价PDE及短期风险中性偏度展开。

Conclusion: 该文刻画了隐藏状态学习中Epstein-Zin经济的均衡价格结构，说明估值因子在正则条件下被均衡固定为常数，并提供了两状态模型的解析与期权定价工具，把信念动态引入波动率与短期收益偏度。

Abstract: We study a representative-agent Epstein-Zin economy with geometric dividends and a hidden finite-state Markov drift. We allow the price-dividend ratio to contain an additional positive, absolutely continuous valuation factor and, within the class $\mathfrak C$ defined below and under the regularity, admissibility, and positivity conditions of our main theorem, equilibrium forces this factor to be constant, yielding belief-Markovian prices. In the two-state case, under the stated positivity condition and strictly positive transition intensities, we prove existence, uniqueness, endpoint smoothness, interior analyticity, and uniform bounds for the positive solution of the pricing equation. For $0<θ\leq1$, this solution supports an equilibrium under any continuous short rate satisfying the model's one-sided portfolio condition. Finally, in the two-state subregion $η>0$, we derive belief-dependent stock volatility, a European option-pricing PDE, and a leading short-maturity conditional risk-neutral log-return skewness expansion.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"稀疏先验"概念与"稀疏维度"度量，证明在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并给出TV距离下匹配的上界，表明合适先验可克服对样本量n的维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI的分布学习理论保证随维度d退化为O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})，虽为极小极大最优，但作者认为这些界过于悲观，因为光滑性假设不足以刻画实际应用中分布的结构。

Method: 引入稀疏先验类，定义"稀疏维度"k作为所有分布空间上先验稀疏性的度量；在k-稀疏先验下推导贝叶斯风险下界，并在温和附加假设下为TV距离证明匹配上界；同时证明贝叶斯框架下分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下，k-稀疏先验的分布学习贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))；在温和附加假设下，TV距离取得（渐近于n、k，忽略对数项）匹配的上界；结果同样适用于学习采样。

Conclusion: k仍可依赖维度d或内在维度，但结果表明在合适的先验下学习可以克服关于样本量n依赖的维度灾难。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [183] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端BI基准BI-Bench，发现前沿LLM准确率不足50%，并设计工具增强的BI-Agent及后训练框架，实现最高40个百分点提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI流程需用户手动完成识别表、数据转换、构建连接等繁琐准备步骤，复杂耗时。受LLM处理数据能力启发，研究其端到端回答BI问题的能力。

Method: 从公开来源采集真实BI项目，人工提取(问题,标准答案)对构建BI-Bench；设计工具增强BI-Agent，将BI工作流分解为搜索、连接、转换等子任务并编排专业数据管理方法；开发后训练框架，从真实项目合成训练轨迹，进行SFT和RL训练。

Result: 前沿LLM在BI-Bench上表现不佳，准确率低于50%；BI-Agent配合原始LLM最高提升40个百分点，后训练后最高再提升30个百分点。

Conclusion: 在复杂BI工作流中，结合工具增强推理与领域特定后训练至关重要，并为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [184] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA提出一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构，通过从query表示动态预测上下文阈值，在保持稠密模型质量的同时实现硬件加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模KV缓存导致长上下文解码的内存带宽瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法依赖刚性启发式规则，会丢弃必要上下文，造成质量下降。

Method: ETA从query表示预测动态上下文阈值，训练时对低于阈值的logits进行乘法抑制而非删除，形成平滑均匀注意力下限以学习策略并避免表示坍缩。推理时可硬剪枝无信息KV块，并吸收粗粒度GPU块选择误纳的token。另实现Triton定制解码核，用缓存的几何-概率界以O(1)时间筛选KV块。还引入离线校准算法，冻结每头常量阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B预训练ETA在约85%训练稀疏度和约38%活跃解码密度下，在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文检索上媲美稠密注意力。Triton解码核在最长512K序列上实现相比FlashAttention-2最高2.5倍墙钟加速。校准后额外减少27%注意力计算。

Conclusion: ETA实现可训练的稀疏注意力，消除初始token注意力汇，兼顾质量与效率，并提供面向特定领域的校准部署方案。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>
